### [1] [IRAH: Hierarchical Agentic Tree Search for Retrieval Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30167)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Aniket Singh,Amir Moghadam,Srividhya Baikadi,Rashmi Shetty*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出IRAH（Information Retrieval Agentic Harness），将LATS-IR（UCT引导的宏观结构+数值参数分层搜索，含自然语言反思）与离线元学习初始化结合，用于有限评估预算下的RAG检索流水线联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为RAG优化IR流水线需在有限评估预算下同时选择结构组件与数值超参数，现有方法（如TPE）难以高效处理这种混合、分层搜索空间。

Method: IRAH = LATS-IR + 离线元学习初始化。LATS-IR采用UCT引导的分层搜索：先在宏观结构层面探索流水线组合，再细化数值参数，并利用自然语言反思引导后续变异、提供可审查的搜索轨迹；元学习初始化提供热启动。

Result: 在3个同分布基准和1个分布外基准上评估：相同初始化下LATS-IR在3个同分布基准中的2个优于TPE，最高提升6.4%，分布外基准保持竞争力；四个基准上热启动IRAH取得最高平均留出nDCG@10（0.4816），并优于冷启动版本。

Conclusion: IRAH是在受限评估预算下具有竞争力的检索流水线优化方法，热启动与分层搜索结合带来稳定收益。

Abstract: Optimizing Information Retrieval (IR) pipelines for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) requires jointly selecting structural components and numerical hyperparameters under limited evaluation budgets. We introduce the Information Retrieval Agentic Harness (IRAH), which combines Language Agent Tree Search for IR (LATS-IR) with offline meta-learned initialization. LATS-IR performs UCT-guided hierarchical search, first exploring macro-structural pipeline choices and then refining numerical parameters, with natural-language reflections to guide subsequent mutations and provide inspectable search traces. We evaluate IRAH on three in-distribution benchmarks and one out of-distribution benchmark to assess generalization under distribution shift. Under the same meta-learned initialization, LATS-IR outperforms TPE on two of three in-distribution benchmarks, with gains of up to 6.4%, and remains competitive on the out-of-distribution benchmark. Across all four benchmarks, warm-started IRAH achieves the highest average held-out nDCG@10 (0.4816) and improves over its cold-start counterpart. These results establish IRAH as a competitive approach to retrieval pipeline optimization under constrained evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [2] [FormBench: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Retrieval in Formulation Patents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30175)
*Vineeth Venugopal,Elsa Olivetti,Jonathan Welch*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出FormBench，一个测试模型从技术文档中检索精确化学信息能力的基准，包含55,352条查询与两个约1M段落的语料变体，并评测10种模型配置，发现商业API表现极差，TAPT微调的Qwen3-Embed与BM25表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索评测缺乏针对技术文档中精确化学信息检索的基准，且难以区分模型的细粒度判别能力，因此需要一个带有多级困难负样本、可衡量模型区分力的评测平台。

Method: 构建FormBench：55,352条查询，配两个约1M段落语料变体（C0随机干扰项、C1结构相近干扰项）；每条查询配锚点段落、硬负例、软负例，负例基于材料本体通过实体重叠评分生成；评测10种配置，涵盖商业API编码器（OpenAI、Voyage）、开源编码器（Nomic、GTE-large、Qwen3-Embed）及其TAPT变体；并进行语料规模消融与分类/聚类任务对比。

Result: (1) 商业API表现极差（OpenAI-3-large在C1上0.009，C0上0.213）；(2) TAPT将Qwen3-Embed提升至C1 0.4085、C0 0.4835；(3) BM25仍具竞争力（C1 0.375，C0 0.467），仅被TAPT-Qwen3超越；(4) 语料规模显著影响检索行为；(5) API失败根源在于域内几何坍缩；(6) 在分类与聚类任务上API反而优于TAPT变体。

Conclusion: FormBench以BEIR兼容格式在huggingface.co/datasets/vinven7/FormBench以CC-BY-4.0发布，揭示了商业嵌入模型在专业化学检索中的严重缺陷及其几何成因，同时表明任务自适应微调可显著提升开源编码器性能。

Abstract: We introduce FormBench, a platform for testing the ability of models to retrieve precise chemical information from technical documents. FormBench contains 55,352 queries and two retrieval corpus variants each with ~1M passages: FormBench-Random (C0), with randomly sampled distractors, and FormBench-Structured (C1), with structurally proximate distractors. Each query is paired with an anchor passage, a hard negative, and a soft negative - derived from a materials ontology through entity overlap scoring, providing a deeper graded signal on a model's discriminatory power. We evaluate 10 model configurations spanning commercial API encoders (OpenAI, Voyage), open-source encoders (Nomic, GTE-large, Qwen3-Embed), and their task-adaptive fine-tuned (TAPT) variants. We find: (1) commercial APIs score very poorly across all configurations (OpenAI-3-large: 0.009 on C1, 0.213 on C0); (2) TAPT on FormBench triplets lifts Qwen3-Embed to 0.4085 on C1, 0.4835 on C0; (3) BM25 remains competitive (0.375 on C1, 0.467 on C0), exceeded only by TAPT-Qwen3; (4) corpus size has a significant impact on retrieval behavior, as demonstrated through a corpus-size ablation; (5) the API failure traces to within-domain geometric collapse; (6) APIs outperform even the TAPT variants on classification and clustering tasks. FormBench is released in BEIR-compatible format at huggingface.co/datasets/vinven7/FormBench under CC-BY-4.0.

</details>


### [3] [FlexVDR: Learning to Allocate Matryoshka Representation Budgets for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30177)
*Haowen Xiang,Yibo Yan,Xin Zou,Shuliang Liu,Yi Cao,Mingdong Ou,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FlexVDR：在单一检索器内对编码深度、嵌入宽度与token数构建联合路径族，用预算分配器依据查询早期特征预测各路径检索效用并结合可配置资源偏好选择路径，实现查询自适应预算分配，在ViDoRe V1/V2/V3上以五个骨干网络验证可在保持较多原生检索质量的同时为单个查询选用紧凑路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉文档检索依赖多向量表示以捕获页面细粒度证据，但编码与匹配开销高昂。现有压缩与弹性表示方法虽提供更小配置，却未解决“查询应使用哪种配置”的决策问题；且该分配需在付出完整计算代价前预估替代表示的检索价值，并兼顾不同资源偏好。

Method: 在单一检索器内构建覆盖编码深度、嵌入宽度、token数的联合路径族；以检索监督与蒸馏训练紧凑路径，并用原生旁路保留全预算参考；设计Budget Allocator，从查询早期特征预测各路径的检索效用，将其与可配置资源偏好结合以选择路径。

Result: 在ViDoRe V1、V2、V3公开集上、跨五个骨干网络的实验表明：FlexVDR在保留大部分原生检索质量的同时，能为单个查询选择紧凑路径；通过可配置资源偏好，同一检索器无需重训练或切换模型即可调整计算量。

Conclusion: 将预算分配视为查询级决策而非仅提供多档配置，可在不牺牲过多检索质量的前提下显著节省计算，并实现单一模型内的弹性资源调节。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval relies on rich multi-vector representations to capture fine-grained evidence in document pages, but encoding and matching these representations can be computationally expensive. Existing compression and elastic representation methods expose smaller configurations, yet making multiple budgets available does not determine which configuration a query should use. The allocation decision must estimate the retrieval value of alternative representations before incurring their full computation, while accommodating different resource preferences. We introduce FlexVDR, a framework for query-adaptive budget allocation within a single retriever. We construct a joint family of routes over encoder depth, embedding width, and token count. Retrieval supervision and distillation train the compact routes, while a native bypass preserves the full-budget reference. A Budget Allocator predicts route-specific retrieval utility from early query features and combines these predictions with a configurable resource preference to select a route. Experiments across five backbones on ViDoRe V1, V2, and V3 public show that FlexVDR retains much of the native retrieval quality while selecting compact routes for individual queries. A configurable resource preference allows the same retriever to adjust its computation without retraining or switching models.

</details>


### [4] [Association between Player Perception and AI Disclosure on Steam: An Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30178)
*Fabian Wiesinger,Johanna Pirker,Denis Helic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文分析Steam评论，考察带有AI披露的游戏是否呈现不同的评论模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在游戏开发中快速普及，玩家反应成为争论核心，但缺乏关于玩家如何看待AI披露的实证认识。

Method: 构建跨多个披露分组的Steam评论数据集，采用关键词分析、词频分析与特征词分析等语言学方法，考察与AI相关的讨论。

Result: 已披露使用AI的游戏，其负面评论中与AI相关的批评水平更高。

Conclusion: 研究为理解玩家对AI披露的感知提供了初步探索性基础。

Abstract: The use of generative AI in game development is growing rapidly and is the subject of intense debate in which player reactions play a central role. This paper contributes to this discussion by analyzing Steam reviews to examine whether games with AI disclosure are associated with different review patterns. Using a dataset across multiple disclosure groups, we apply linguistic analysis including keyword-based, word frequency, and characteristic word analysis to investigate the AI-related discussion on Steam. Our results show that AI-disclosed games are associated with higher levels of AI-related criticism in negative reviews. Our findings provide a first exploratory step towards understanding how players perceive AI disclosure.

</details>


### [5] [QProtFuse: Query-Guided Layer Fusion for Retrieval-Augmented Protein Function Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30180)
*Mianzhi Pan,Peishuo Liu,Yawen Ouyang,Botian Wang,Yiming Rong,Hao Zhou,Jianbing Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出QProtFuse，用查询自适应融合蛋白语言模型（PLM）多层特征，并联合学习蛋白与注释检索，提升基于RAG的蛋白功能描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从氨基酸序列预测蛋白功能对理解生物过程至关重要。LLM可灵活生成自然语言功能描述，但蛋白理解能力有限，因此需借助RAG。现有蛋白RAG方法主要扩展外部检索信号，却忽视了单个PLM内部编码的丰富知识；且检索性能随PLM层与生物任务而变化，仅用最后一层并非最优。

Method: 提出QProtFuse：以查询自适应地融合多层蛋白特征；通过模态内与跨模态对比目标，联合学习蛋白检索与注释检索。

Result: 在Prot-Inst-OOD上取得最佳蛋白与注释检索分数，结合注释重排达到16.4% Recall@1；下游RAG功能描述质量提升，LLM-as-a-judge得分在Qwen3-8B上为6.22/10、DeepSeek-V4-Pro上为6.68/10，均为最高。消融与权重分析验证了查询引导融合与互补训练目标对挖掘PLM内部知识的价值。

Conclusion: 查询引导的多层特征融合与多目标联合训练能有效利用PLM内部知识，改善检索与下游RAG功能描述。

Abstract: Predicting protein functions from amino acid sequences is essential to understanding biological processes. Large language models (LLMs) offer flexible function descriptions to natural-language queries, yet their limited protein understanding motivates retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Existing protein RAG methods primarily expand external retrieval signals while overlooking the rich knowledge encoded within a single protein language model (PLM). We show that retrieval performance varies across PLM layers and biological tasks, making conventional final-layer selection suboptimal. To this end, we propose QProtFuse, which uses queries to adaptively fuse multi-layer protein features and jointly learns protein and annotation retrieval through intra-modal and cross-modal contrastive objectives. On Prot-Inst-OOD, QProtFuse achieves the best protein and annotation retrieval scores, reaching 16.4\% Recall@1 with annotation reranking. These gains improve downstream RAG-based function descriptions, yielding the highest LLM-as-a-judge scores for both Qwen3-8B (6.22/10) and DeepSeek-V4-Pro (6.68/10). Ablations and weight analyses demonstrate the value of query-guided fusion and complementary training objectives for exploiting a PLM's internal knowledge.

</details>


### [6] [Deep Search in Real-World Web Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30181)
*Xiaomin Yu,Hongliang Chen,Zhe Huang,Shilin Yan,Shao-Rong Su,Xuqiao Ma,Feiyang Wang,Jiayu Chen,Jiwei Fang,Yu Yan,Yini Huang,Chen Chen,Ruoxi Cheng,Yuzhi He,Yuhui Zhang,Xiaoxing Hu,Chen Liu,Jianbo Lin,Yijiang Li,Xiaobin Hu,Menglong Li,Zhuoran Lu,Weishi Wang,Zhongqi Yue,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 OpenDS 任务与 OpenDS-World 基准（200 道需多源证据整合的开放网络深度搜索题），在统一工具环境下评测 12 个前沿模型，最佳准确率 39.0%，揭示工具协调与长程搜索管理的持续挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Deep Search agent 评测多基于静态检索环境，而真实开放网络的信息分散于多源、多模态与异构格式中，且需复杂交互机制，agent 必须在执行过程中自主搜索、访问并整合证据；这一端到端研究能力缺乏充分评测。

Method: 构建 OpenDS 任务：要求 agent 自主探索真实在线网络，通过网页搜索、浏览器交互与证据处理，从多个独立来源发现并关联证据，最终给出唯一事实性答案。据此构建 OpenDS-World 基准，含 200 道高难度任务，覆盖多种信息模态、异构文件格式与交互式访问需求，并辅以人工独立校验以保证证据有效性与答案唯一性。在统一工具环境与执行协议下评测 12 个前沿模型，并分析工具使用、搜索成本、终止行为与失败恢复。

Result: 最佳模型准确率为 39.0%；人类在 1000 次独立尝试上的准确率为 21.1%。工具使用、搜索成本、终止行为与失败恢复分析显示各模型研究策略差异显著，且在工具协调与长程搜索管理方面仍存在持续挑战。

Conclusion: OpenDS-World 为系统评估与推进 agent 在真实开放网络环境中的自主深度搜索能力提供了统一评测平台。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in Deep Search agents, their ability to conduct end-to-end research in real-world open Web environments remains insufficiently evaluated. Unlike static retrieval settings, information on the real-world Web is distributed across diverse sources, modalities, and heterogeneous formats, often involving complex interaction mechanisms that require agents to autonomously search for, access, and integrate relevant evidence during execution. To address this gap, we introduce the OpenDS task, designed to evaluate agents' ability to perform multi-step deep search in the live, open Web. This task requires agents to autonomously explore the real Web, discover and connect relevant evidence from multiple independent sources through Web search, browser interaction, and evidence processing, and ultimately determine a unique factual answer. Building on this task formulation, we construct OpenDS-World, a benchmark comprising 200 challenging tasks spanning diverse information modalities, heterogeneous file formats, and interactive access requirements, with independent human verification to ensure evidence validity and answer uniqueness. We evaluate 12 frontier models under a unified tool environment and execution protocol. The best-performing model achieves 39.0% accuracy, while human evaluation across 1,000 independent attempts yields 21.1% accuracy. Further analyses of tool usage, search cost, termination behavior, and failure recovery reveal substantial differences in research strategies across models, as well as persistent challenges in tool coordination and long-horizon search management. OpenDS-World provides a unified evaluation platform for systematically assessing and advancing agents' autonomous deep search capabilities in real-world open Web environments.

</details>


### [7] [FAIRGate: Agentic Runtime Policy Selection and Verified Repair for Fairer Recommendation Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30359)
*Navkar Jain,Enock Ayiku,Kenneth Fletcher*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的交付控制器 FAIRGate，在基础排序器与最终 top-K 列表之间加入智能体策略选择层，从预定义公平策略库中选择策略并由确定性执行器与验证器保证公平可度量、有界且可重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平感知推荐系统通常在训练时编码单一公平目标，或在打分后套用固定重排规则；但部署中候选池、物品元数据、曝光债务、用户偏好历史与效用预算随请求动态变化，因此合适的公平动作应随请求而变。

Method: \nFAIRGate 为模型无关的交付控制器，位于基础排序器与最终 top-K 列表之间。智能体不从头定义公平目标，而是从预定义策略库中选择策略，涵盖长尾曝光、物品群体曝光平衡、集中度控制、校准保护与弃权。随后由确定性执行器通过约束修复施加所选策略，验证器仅在相关性、可行性、元数据、校准与审计约束均满足时才接受结果。语言模型组件仅接收经核验的审计轨迹，在排序决策固定后生成可溯源到证据的叙述。

Result: 摘要未提供定量实验结果；作者结论为：该设计将运行期责任赋予智能体，同时使公平执行保持可度量、有界且可重放。

Conclusion: \nFAIRGate 通过“策略库+智能体选择+确定性执行+约束验证+事后可溯源叙述”的分层架构，实现随请求自适应的公平交付，并避免语言模型参与排序决策带来的不可控性。

Abstract: Fairness-aware recommender systems often encode one fairness objective during training or apply a fixed re-ranking rule after scoring. In deployment, however, the appropriate fairness action can vary across requests because the candidate pool, item metadata, exposure debt, user preference history, and utility budget all change at runtime. We propose FAIRGate, a model-agnostic delivery controller that places an agentic policy-selection layer between a base ranker and the final top-K list. The agent does not define fairness objectives from scratch. It chooses from a predefined policy library covering long-tail exposure, item-group exposure balance, concentration control, calibration protection, and abstention. A deterministic executor then applies the selected policy through constrained repair, and a validator accepts the result only when relevance, feasibility, metadata, calibration, and audit constraints are satisfied. The final language-model component receives only the verified audit trace and produces evidence-linked narration after the ranking decision is fixed. This design gives the agent runtime responsibility while keeping fairness enforcement measurable, bounded, and replayable.

</details>


### [8] [When Agentic Retrieval Fails Silently: Framework and Production Evidence from a Creative AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30423)
*Yan,Liang,Anuj Agarwal,Aditya Reddy Cheruku*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出"归因鸿沟"概念并设计LARA三层评估框架，用四字段血缘schema把检索质量、参与/结果、产品成功解耦评估，并在Adobe Express上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTR、MRR、behavioral NDCG等传统指标默认"用户直接看到排序结果并据此行动"，但在agentic助手场景中该假设在三方面失效：who（agent先于用户读取结果）、when（用户行动可能滞后数轮或跨会话）、what caused it（结果混合了检索内容与模型自身知识），导致检索贡献无法归因。

Method: 提出Layered Agentic Retrieval Assessment（LARA），将评估分为三层独立指标：检索质量、参与度与结果、产品成功，层间通过四字段血缘（lineage）schema连接，无需新增建模。以Adobe Express为案例：同一套检索基础设施同时服务图库搜索界面与agentic助手（后者会话量约为前者1/8，用户群相同），据此剖析归因鸿沟的动机。

Result: Chat用户的重构（reformulation）率高于图库用户（44.4% vs. 33.1%），原因是chat中的重构用于细化已生成输出，而非单纯修正检索查询。

Conclusion: LARA为检索恢复了归因能力，使检索在agentic链路中的真实贡献可被独立度量。

Abstract: CTR, MRR, and behavioral NDCG assume the user sees ranked results and acts on them. In an agentic assistant, a generation layer consumes retrieval output first, and that assumption breaks in three ways. The agent reads the results before the user does (who). The user's action can come several turns or sessions after retrieval (when). Outcomes blend retrieved content with the model's own knowledge (what caused it). We call this the attribution gap. We propose Layered Agentic Retrieval Assessment (LARA), which measures retrieval quality, engagement and outcome, and product success as three independent layers joined by a four-field lineage schema that requires no new modeling. We examine the motivation on Adobe Express, where one retrieval infrastructure serves a gallery search surface and an agentic assistant at roughly one-eighth its session volume for the same user population. Chat users reformulate more than gallery users (44.4% vs. 33.1%) because chat reformulation refines generated output. LARA restores attribution for the retrieval.

</details>


### [9] [Getting a GRIP on Graph-Based Search for Learned Sparse Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30425)
*Vihan Lakshman,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出图索引系统 GRIP，将倒排索引优势融入图检索，在 MS MARCO v2 上较 Seismic 提速 2.5×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区普遍认为倒排索引在学习型稀疏检索（LSR）上优于图索引，但该文质疑这一默认结论，认为图索引的劣势源于直接套用标准 ANN 图索引，而非本质缺陷。

Method: 提出三项加速技术并整合为 GRIP：(i) 基于 term 的入口点种子选择；(ii) 基于稀疏 sketch 的近似路由；(iii) 基于稀疏分区的图构建。

Result: 在 MS MARCO v2（1.38 亿段落）上用 SPLADE 嵌入，95% recall@10 下相较 Seismic 延迟降低 2.5×。

Conclusion: 图 ANN 索引经针对性改造后可超越倒排索引，倒排索引并非 LSR 部署的必然首选。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval (LSR) has recently gained traction in the information retrieval community for the ability to combine the generalization capabilities of neural representations with the interpretability of keyword-based search. A number of recent works have demonstrated that inverted indexes, when appropriately modified for learned sparse embeddings, achieve state-of-the-art recall-latency tradeoffs for LSR, often outperforming graph-based indexes by substantial margins in latency at a fixed recall target. As a result, the community has generally concluded that graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search is inferior to inverted indexes for LSR. This paper challenges this conventional wisdom. Our key insight is that, while applying standard graph-based ANN indexes out-of-the-box to sparse vectors leads to uncompetitive performance, we can overcome these shortcomings by infusing the strengths of inverted indexes into graph-based search. In particular, we introduce three techniques to accelerate graph-based LSR: (i) term-based entry point seeding, (ii) approximate routing via sparse sketching, and (iii) sparse partition-based graph construction. We combine these techniques into a new graph-based ANN system for LSR we call GRIP. On the MS MARCO v2 benchmark (138M passages) with SPLADE embeddings, GRIP achieves a $2.5\times$ latency improvement over Seismic, a state-of-the-art inverted index tailored for LSR, at 95\% recall@10. Ultimately, our results call into question the default choice of inverted indexes for deploying learned sparse retrieval.

</details>


### [10] [The Fusion Tax: A Diagnostic Analysis of When Hybrid Retrieval Hurts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30612)
*Aaditya Chauhan,Arun Agarwal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 混合检索（BM25+稠密模型，常用RRF融合）在聚合指标上普遍优于单一检索器，但本文将其"不融合"视为一等结果，系统量化融合在个体查询上的代价，并提出"融合税"概念及其判定准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件文档与综述普遍推荐混合检索（尤其RRF），依据是聚合指标提升。但作者关心：在真实部署场景下，融合对单个查询造成多大损失，以及在什么条件下应选择不融合。

Method: 在7个BEIR数据集上，采用Anserini BM25、5个稠密编码器（22M–596M参数、跨3代模型）、SPLADE学习稀疏替代方案与cross-encoder重排；比较3种融合函数，以"稠密加权分数插值"为主要对象；统计逐查询的排序损失比例，并做损失厌恶的风险敏感评估与留一语料库交叉验证确定融合权重；分析损失的来源（召回vs排序）、触发条件（仅单检索器覆盖的查询）、跨编码器与SPLADE的稳定性，以及可预测性与跨语料迁移性。

Result: 最优方法（稠密加权分数插值）仍在13–48%查询上把相关文档排在两组件中较优者之下，在8–21%查询上低于可部署的纯稠密检索器（留一验证权重下为14–48%与11–25%）；尽管在所有语料上聚合指标优于纯稠密；在7个语料中的6个上，风险敏感评估下损失显著。RRF在稠密检索明显更强时税负更高，仅在两组件均衡时优于纯稠密。该损失主要为排序效应、聚合召回基本保留，最易出现在仅一个检索器覆盖的查询上，并跨编码器梯度与SPLADE替代持续存在。融合失败在同语料内弱可预测，但跨语料迁移不可靠，因预测特征随稀疏-稠密平衡而变号。cross-encoder仅在其自身可靠的场景下挽回部分税负。

Conclusion: 混合检索的聚合收益掩盖了逐查询的系统性损失（"融合税"）。应将融合决策绑定到目标语料实测的稀疏-稠密平衡：稠密明显占优时慎用或不用RRF，仅在组件均衡时融合更稳健，并据此形成部署指南。

Abstract: Hybrid retrieval, which fuses a sparse lexical retriever with a dense neural one, is widely recommended in the software documentation surveyed in September 2026, usually with reciprocal rank fusion (RRF), on the strength of aggregate gains. We ask what fusion costs on individual queries, treating "do not fuse" as a first-class outcome, in the setting in which hybrid retrieval is deployed: a standard Anserini BM25, five dense encoders spanning 22M-596M parameters and three model generations, a learned-sparse (SPLADE) alternative, and a cross-encoder second stage, on seven BEIR datasets and three fusion functions. Even the best method, dense-weighted score interpolation, ranks relevant documents below the better of its two components on 13-48% of queries and below the deployable dense-only retriever on 8-21% (14-48% and 11-25% at a weight selected under leave-one-corpus-out validation), while beating dense-only in aggregate on every corpus with the primary encoder; the downside is significant under loss-averse risk-sensitive evaluation on six of the seven corpora. We call this loss the fusion tax. It is mostly a ranking effect that preserves recall in aggregate, it is most likely on the queries that only one retriever covers, and it persists across the encoder ladder and the SPLADE substitution. RRF carries the higher tax wherever the dense retriever is clearly stronger and only beats dense-only where the two components are balanced. Fusion failure is weakly predictable within a corpus but transfers unreliably across corpora, because the features that predict it change sign with the sparse-dense balance. A cross-encoder recovers part of the tax only where it is itself reliable. We distill these findings into guidance that keys the fusion decision to the measured sparse-dense balance of the target corpus.

</details>


### [11] [OneLatent: Latent Reasoning for Efficient Foundation Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30675)
*Hao Jiang,Peiru Du,Pengfei Yao,Mengting Li,Siyuan Lou,Kuo Cai,Sheng Yu,Qiang Luo,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出OneLatent潜空间推理框架，将显式CoT压缩为可学习潜token，在快手大规模推荐上超越显式CoT并实现17倍以上推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的基础推荐模型采用Think-then-Answer的显式CoT提升效果，但生成长推理链带来高昂推理开销，且依赖人工模板，难以刻画多样且动态的用户兴趣。

Method: 1) Multi-View Adaptive CoT（MV-ACoT）：多视角探索用户兴趣并按样本自适应调整推理复杂度，构建多样化高质量教师监督；2) 三阶段潜token对齐范式：在预训练FRM上逐步将CoT轨迹内化为可学习潜token；3) 多阶段课程式后训练：激活潜token推理以适配下游推荐任务。

Result: 在工业级快手数据集与公开Kuaishou LLM-Rec基准上稳定优于显式CoT方法与传统基线；相较FRM的Think与No-Think变体，SID@64分别提升17.44%与9.33%，在线推理吞吐提升逾17倍；并搭建生产级实时服务系统，快手本地服务广告场景A/B测试相对OneRec、OneReason等强基线估计带来9.6%收入提升。

Conclusion: 以潜token替代显式推理链可在保持甚至提升推荐效果的同时大幅降低推理开销，具备工业级可部署性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning capabilities, motivating their use as the backbone of foundation recommendation models (FRMs). Existing methods enhance recommendations through explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning under a Think-then-Answer paradigm. However, explicit CoT incurs substantial inference overhead by generating lengthy reasoning traces and relies on manually designed templates that struggle to capture diverse, dynamic user interests. We propose OneLatent, an efficient latent reasoning framework that compresses explicit reasoning traces into several learnable latent tokens, enabling Latent-Reason-then-Answer inference without generating verbose traces. OneLatent first introduces Multi-View Adaptive CoT (MV-ACoT), which creates diverse, high-quality teacher-generated supervision by exploring user interests from multiple perspectives and automatically adapting reasoning complexity to each instance. Building on pretrained FRMs, it then uses a three-stage latent-token alignment paradigm to progressively internalize CoT traces into learnable latent tokens. Finally, a multistage curriculum-based post-training strategy activates latent-token reasoning for downstream recommendation tasks. Experiments on an industrial-scale Kuaishou dataset and the public Kuaishou LLM-Rec benchmark show that OneLatent consistently outperforms explicit CoT-based methods and traditional baselines. Compared with the Think and No-Think variants of FRMs, OneLatent improves SID@64 by 17.44% and 9.33%, respectively, while achieving over 17x higher online inference throughput. We further develop a production serving system for scalable, real-time FRM inference. An online A/B test in Kuaishou's local-services advertising scenario shows that deploying OneLatent with this system yields an estimated 9.6% revenue lift over strong online baselines, including OneRec and OneReason.

</details>


### [12] [Lychee: Certified Screening of Inverted Lists for High-Recall Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30697)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Lychee: 一种基于GPU的倒排索引向量检索系统，利用锚点阈值在张量核上对探测列表进行"认证排除"筛选，只对少数候选读取全精度向量并计算精确距离，从而在保持高召回（0.999–0.9999）的同时大幅降低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高召回近邻搜索在刷脸比对、欺诈检测、医学影像等场景中要求漏检即风险，需0.999–0.9999召回，但成本须远低于精确搜索。倒排索引靠多探测列表提召回，却需对每个探测向量全精度读取打分，吞吐与探测列表数成反比；图索引难以达到该召回，量化列表还需用全向量重排。核心痛点是：缺乏不读取向量即可判定其无法进入top-k的机制。

Method: 提出"认证排除"（certified exclusion）：借助精确搜索的认证下界，对几乎每个探测向量在读取前判定其不可能进入探测列表的top-k。实现为Lychee——GPU倒排索引搜索：以同列表中锚点导出阈值，在张量核上对探测列表进行批量筛选，仅对少数幸存者计算精确距离。并证明其返回结果恰为探测列表的k个最近向量（即全精度倒排搜索的答案），每查询仅读取数百个全向量，与探测列表数无关。

Result: 在11个数据集（100万至10亿向量）上与10个GPU/CPU基线对比：200探测列表等召回下，Lychee比IVF-Flat快9.7–117倍（≤1亿向量）；达到CAGRA与HNSW无法企及的召回水平；在超1000万向量时比认证排除的精确搜索快5–18倍；4块GPU上以0.9999召回搜索10亿向量，在BIGANN-1B上比IVF-Flat快44.5倍。

Conclusion: 通过认证排除将"读取并打分"的ILP瓶颈转化为廉价的张量核筛选，Lychee在严格保持倒排搜索精确语义与高召回的同时实现数量级加速，证明高召回近邻搜索可兼顾精度与吞吐。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor search over embedding vectors matches faces against watchlists, transactions against known fraud patterns, and medical images against confirmed cases, where a missed neighbor is a missed threat, fraud, or diagnosis. Because an approximate index cannot report which queries lost a true neighbor, these applications require recall of 0.999 to 0.9999, at a cost far below that of exact search over billions of vectors. Inverted-file indexes reach high recall by probing more lists, but they read and score every probed vector at full precision, so that throughput falls in inverse proportion to the number of probed lists. Graph indexes saturate below these targets, whereas quantized lists must rerank their candidates with the full vectors. Our insight is to decide for almost every probed vector that it cannot enter the top k of the probed lists without reading the vector, through the certified lower bound of exact search by certified exclusion. This paper turns this insight into Lychee, a GPU inverted-file search that screens the probed lists on tensor cores under a threshold from anchors of the same lists and computes exact distances only for the few survivors. We prove that Lychee returns exactly the k nearest vectors of the probed lists, the answer of an inverted-file search at full precision, while it reads a few hundred full vectors per query regardless of the number of probed lists. On eleven datasets from one million to one billion vectors and against ten GPU and CPU baselines, Lychee is 9.7 to 117 times faster than IVF-Flat at equal recall with 200 probed lists on datasets of up to 100 million vectors. It also reaches recall levels that CAGRA and HNSW do not reach and outruns exact search by certified exclusion by 5 to 18 times above ten million vectors. On four GPUs, Lychee searches one billion vectors at recall 0.9999, 44.5 times faster than IVF-Flat on BIGANN-1B.

</details>


### [13] [Automated Enrichment Analysis with GraphRAG: Efficient Multi-Database ORA Extraction and Biological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30710)
*Guangcan Li,Shuyuan Zheng,Jiayao Liu,Shigeto Seno,Makoto Onizuka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出GBOR-RAG框架，将异源基因集数据库统一为"术语–基因"知识图谱，经图式ORA打分后检索紧凑连通证据子图，用于约束LLM生成可溯源生物学解释，并引入声明级忠实度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统过表征分析（ORA）输出分散于多个基因集数据库，且以扁平术语列表呈现，掩盖了相关富集信号之间的基因级关联，导致结果碎片化、缺乏可解释性与可溯源性。

Method: 1）构建统一的术语–基因异构知识图谱，以共享基因连接跨库相关术语；2）在图谱上进行ORA打分；3）检索保持跨库关系与基因级支持的紧凑连通证据子图；4）以子图作为条件输入驱动LLM生成生物学解释；5）提出声明级忠实度指标，评估生成声明是否由显式证据支持；6）可选叠加PubMed文献层。

Result: 图式ORA与传统ORA高度一致（平均Pearson r=0.997），查询时间中位数降低37倍（最高48倍）；检索子图平均36.8个节点，整体整合得分最高（4.12）；GBOR-RAG的声明级忠实度最高（0.90）；加入PubMed文献层后整体解释质量由4.17提升至4.75。

Conclusion: GBOR-RAG在保持传统ORA结果一致性的同时显著提升效率，并通过证据子图实现跨库整合与基因级可溯源的LLM解释生成；文献层叠加可进一步增强解释质量，为ORA结果解读提供了集成化、可追踪的范式。

Abstract: Over-representation analysis (ORA) is widely used to interpret gene-expression studies, but its outputs are often fragmented across gene-set databases and presented as flat term lists that obscure gene-level links among related enrichment signals. We propose Graph-Based Over-Representation Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GBOR-RAG), an integrated and traceable framework for ORA interpretation. GBOR-RAG represents heterogeneous gene-set databases as a unified term--gene knowledge graph, where shared genes connect related terms across sources. After graph-based ORA scoring, the framework retrieves a compact connected evidence subgraph that preserves both cross-database relationships and gene-level support. The subgraph conditions an LLM to generate biological interpretations, and we introduce a claim-level faithfulness metric to assess whether generated claims are supported by explicit evidence. Experiments show that graph-based ORA preserves conventional ORA results with high consistency (mean Pearson $r=0.997$) while reducing query time by a median of $37\times$ (up to $48\times$). The retrieved subgraphs average 36.8 nodes and achieve the highest overall integration score (4.12), and GBOR-RAG attains the highest claim-level faithfulness (0.90) among the compared systems. Adding a PubMed literature layer further improves the overall interpretation quality from 4.17 to 4.75.

</details>


### [14] [Bezier-Guided Adaptive Flow for Cross-domain Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30825)
*Ziang Lu,Yu Zhang,Xinzhe Jiang,Lei Sang,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出BGAFlow框架，通过个性化迁移范围控制与状态自适应语义演化，缓解跨域序列推荐中用户偏好一致性差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域序列推荐方法依赖全局对齐目标或粗粒度迁移策略，忽视用户跨域偏好一致性的差异，易引入无关信息且语义表示缺乏个性化灵活性。

Method: 1）借助LLM推断用户画像，进行统一双域预训练以建立共享语义空间；2）基于潜在兴趣重叠度为每个用户估计个性化“视界”（horizon），确定迁移程度；3）构建条件Bezier曲线作为几何参考，学习残差流逐步精化轨迹；4）引入动态偏好反馈调整迁移方向，并以推荐监督与轨迹正则化保证有效平滑的适应。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，BGAFlow一致优于SOTA基线，验证了个性化迁移控制与状态自适应语义演化的有效性。

Conclusion: 用户级迁移范围控制结合状态自适应的流式语义演化，是提升跨域序列推荐性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation aims to alleviate data sparsity and cold-start problems by transferring useful preference knowledge across domains. However, users exhibit varying degrees of preference consistency across domains, while existing methods often rely on global alignment objectives or coarse-grained transfer strategies. Such approaches may introduce irrelevant information and provide limited flexibility in adapting semantic representations to individual user preferences. To address these challenges, we propose BGAFlow, a personalized framework that combines state-adaptive semantic evolution with user-specific transfer control. BGAFlow first establishes a shared semantic space through unified dual-domain pretraining with user profiles inferred by a large language model (LLM). Based on latent interest overlap, it then estimates a personalized horizon to determine the appropriate transfer extent for each user. To enable semantic adaptation, BGAFlow constructs a conditional Bezier curve as a geometric reference and learns a residual flow that progressively refines the trajectory based on intermediate preference states. The learned flow incorporates dynamic preference feedback to adjust transfer directions, while recommendation supervision and trajectory regularization encourage effective and smooth adaptation. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that BGAFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating the benefits of personalized transfer control and state-adaptive semantic evolution.

</details>


### [15] [UserArena: Benchmarking Interactive User Simulation for Long-Horizon Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30932)
*Jujia Zhao,Jiakai Tang,Jing Yu,Wen Chen,Jian Wu,Han Zhu,Fei Sun,Suzan Verberne,Zhaochun Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 UserArena 真实电商交互式基准（64,729 条会话轨迹、11,010 用户、566,102 物品、13 类动作），支持与生产线 A/B 结果对齐的五级离线评估，并提出 On-Policy Trajectory Distillation (OPTD) 提升长时程模拟器保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic 推荐通过一系列依赖上下文的决策实现购物目标，其策略开发与评估依赖能同时刻画交互环境与用户长期行为响应的推荐世界模型；但现有资源缺乏长时程、细粒度真实用户轨迹，也难以支持轨迹级评估及与线上 A/B 结果的离线验证。

Method: 构建 UserArena：真实电商交互式基准，含 64,729 条会话轨迹（11,010 用户、566,102 物品、13 类动作），配套交互环境与受控生产 A/B 结果；设计覆盖输出有效性、局部决策、序列行为、个性化、最终结果五层次的评估协议。并提出 OPTD：利用轨迹级意图在模型自生成 rollout 上提供稠密教师目标，进行轨迹级蒸馏。

Result: 在 UserArena 评估协议下，OPTD 在所评测的模拟器中取得最佳整体保真度，并在离线模拟中最接近复现线上策略效果。

Conclusion: UserArena 为 agentic 推荐提供了可长时程、可对齐线上结果的真实世界评估平台，OPTD 通过轨迹级蒸馏有效缓解长时程模拟的保真度退化，二者共同构成"基准+方法"闭环。

Abstract: Agentic recommendation pursues shopper goals through sequences of context-dependent decisions. Developing and evaluating such policies requires faithful recommendation world models that capture both the interactive shopping environment and users' behavioral responses over long interactions. Existing resources rarely provide long-horizon, fine-grained real-user trajectories or support trajectory-level evaluation and offline validation against production A/B results. We introduce UserArena, a real-world e-commerce interactive benchmark containing 64,729 session trajectories from 11,010 users, spanning 566,102 items and 13 action types. UserArena pairs these trajectories with an interactive environment and supports offline validation against outcomes from a controlled production A/B test, enabling five-level evaluation from output validity and local decisions to sequential behavior, personalization, and outcomes. To improve simulator fidelity over these long-horizon interactions, we further propose On-Policy Trajectory Distillation (OPTD), which uses trajectory-level intent to provide dense teacher targets on student-generated rollouts. Experiments show that OPTD achieves the strongest overall fidelity among the evaluated simulators under UserArena's evaluation protocol and most closely reproduces the production policy effect in offline simulation.

</details>


### [16] [PAIR: Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval for Premium People Recommendation at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30941)
*Zhongkai Shangguan,Yanbin Kang,Yihan Cao,Ruijie Jiang,Qiannan Yin,Chang Yue,Zijian Zhou,Girish Kathalagiri,Changshuai Wei,Zhengming Xing*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 PAIR（Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval）检索框架，用偏好对齐的意图检索替代画像相似度匹配，在 LinkedIn Premium 检索任务上以 0.6B 参数模型取得 0.45 的 MAR@100。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用嵌入基于画像相似度训练，在职业网络中会错排候选：相关关系本质是互补而非对称的（求职者倾向与招聘者互动，而非背景相似的同行）。现有用户推荐依赖画像相似度或共同连接等社交信号，仅在好友导向社交网络有效；职业平台交互是目标驱动、跨角色的，需要基于互补意图匹配。

Method: 借鉴 DPO 的偏好学习视角，但将其落地为面向嵌入的、以曝光为条件的多负样本对比目标（exposure-conditioned multi-negative contrastive objective）；无需显式参考模型，训练简化且可扩展到大规模候选集。同时聚合点击历史并用位置编码刻画时序，在固定历史窗口上实现用户表示的常数代价更新，新交互无需重新编码历史，线上可用轻量 CPU 操作完成个性化更新，避免重复 LLM 推理。

Result: 在 LinkedIn Premium 检索任务上评估 PAIR，0.6B 参数模型达到 MAR@100 = 0.45。

Conclusion: 在职业网络中应以互补意图偏好而非画像相似度建模相关性；PAIR 以简化的对比式偏好学习目标兼顾效果与可扩展性，并通过位置编码历史聚合实现低成本、可实时更新的个性化检索。

Abstract: General-purpose embeddings trained on profile similarity often misrank candidates in professional networks, where relevance is inherently complementary rather than symmetric. For example, job seekers usually engage with recruiters rather than peers with similar credentials. Current user recommendation systems are often built on profile similarity or social signals such as shared connections, which are effective in friend-oriented social networks where users tend to connect with similar people or within existing social circles. However, this does not hold in professional platforms like LinkedIn, where interactions are often goal-driven and cross-role interactions, requiring matching based on complementary intents rather than similarity or social proximity. In this paper, we propose Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval, a retrieval framework that is designed to model user intent preference instead of profile similarity. Our approach is inspired by the preference-learning perspective of Direct Preference Optimization, but instantiates preference learning as an exposure-conditioned multi-negative contrastive objective over embeddings. This formulation simplifies training and scales efficiently to large candidate sets without requiring an explicit reference model. Furthermore, we incorporate user interaction history by aggregating click histories with positional encoding to capture temporal order, which enables constant-cost updates of user representations over a fixed history window, allowing new interactions to be incorporated without re-encoding history. As a result, personalization can be updated efficiently using lightweight CPU operations without repeated LLM inference during serving. We evaluate PAIR on LinkedIn's Premium retrieval task. A 0.6B parameter model achieves a MAR@100 of 0.45.

</details>


### [17] [Formal-Informal Mathematical Alignment through and beyond Human Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31011)
*Noah Gießing,Ankit Satpute,Hamid Rahkooy,André Greiner-Petter,Moritz Schubotz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 构建 Stacks Project 非形式化陈述与 mathlib 形式化陈述的链接数据集，并用检索、LLM 重排序与 LLM 辅助验证发现新的对齐关系，最终将结果融入 MaRDI 知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学库（如 Lean 的 mathlib）提供了机器可验证的数学知识，而非形式化资源与形式化库之间的连接目前稀疏且不成体系；若二者有效关联，可揭示数学概念间的隐式联系并提升现有形式化的可访问性。

Method: 利用专家标注构建 Stacks Project 非形式化陈述到 mathlib 形式化的连接数据集；评估检索（retrieval）、基于 LLM 的重排序（reranking）以及 LLM 辅助验证（LLM-assisted verification）以发现未标注的非形式化—形式化链接；并将新发现的对齐整合进 MaRDI 知识图谱。

Result: 构建了基于专家标注的跨库链接数据集；通过检索加 LLM 重排序与验证流程发现了新的未标注链接；将新对齐关系集成到 MaRDI 知识图谱中，供数学家查找非形式化内容的形式化对应物。

Conclusion: 该工作是形式化与非形式化数学更好整合的初步尝试；作者讨论了使用 LLM 关联形式化与非形式化数学内容时的困难与挑战，并公开了代码与数据以供复现。

Abstract: Formalized mathematics libraries such as Lean's \texttt{mathlib} provide machine-verifiable representations of mathematical knowledge, including definitions, theorems, and their relationships. If effectively connected to informal mathematical resources, formal mathematics could help uncover previously implicit links between mathematical concepts and improve access to existing formalizations. However, connections between informal and formal mathematics libraries are currently sparse and unsystematic. In this paper, we make first strides towards better formal integration. We construct a dataset linking informal statements from the Stacks Project to their formalizations in \texttt{mathlib} by exploiting expert annotations. We evaluate retrieval, LLM-based reranking, and LLM-assisted verification to discover new unannotated informal-to-formal links. To facilitate exploration and reuse of formalized knowledge, we integrated the newly discovered alignments into the MaRDI knowledge graph, enabling mathematicians to find formal counterparts to informal mathematical content. We discuss the difficulties and challenges in using LLMs for linking formal and informal mathematical content. All code and data necessary for reproducing this paper can be found at: https://github.com/AnkitSatpute/Formal-Informal-Mathematical-Alignment

</details>


### [18] [The Delayed Countdown Method for Validating Review-Oriented AI Workflows in eDiscovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31311)
*David D. Lewis,Tara Emory,Lenora Gray,Mark Noel,Jeremy Pickens,Eugene Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Delayed Countdown方法，用于评估面向审阅流程的eDiscovery工作流效果，兼容任意停止规则并能给出有效的频率学置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向审阅的流程已成为法律电子取证中AI辅助文档审阅的主流方式，但如何验证其有效性在技术辅助审阅（TAR）领域长期存在争议且令人困惑。作者旨在厘清该争议的来源并提供实用评估方案。

Method: 提出Delayed Countdown方法：概念上简单实用，可嵌入现有面向审阅的工作流与软件，与任意停止规则兼容，并对有效性产生有效的频率学置信区间；文中给出概念基础、示例计算，并讨论逻辑上不可能的召回值处理。

Result: 该方法与现有工作流和软件易于结合，支持任意停止规则，并能在有效性上给出符合频率学框架的有效置信区间；同时明确了不可能召回值的处理方式。

Conclusion: Delayed Countdown为面向审阅流程的效果验证提供了简洁可行的通用框架，后续工作可聚焦于降低审阅成本。

Abstract: Review-oriented workflows have become the dominant approach to AI-assisted document review in electronic discovery (eDiscovery). How to validate the effectiveness of review-oriented workflows has, however, remained a confusing and controversial topic in technology-assisted review (TAR) in the law. We examine the sources of this controversy, and propose a simple and practical evaluation approach for review-oriented workflows. Our Delayed Countdown method is easily incorporated into existing review-oriented workflows and software, is compatible with arbitrary stopping rules, and produces valid frequentist confidence intervals on effectiveness. We explain the conceptual foundations of the method, provide example computations, discuss the handling of logically impossible recall values, and suggest future work on decreasing review costs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [19] [Deep ReLU Networks Can Fail the Best Approximation Property for Continuous Target Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31264)
*Sebastian Kassing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文构造了 $[-1,1]^2$ 上的连续函数，证明在两层隐藏层结构 $(2,2,1,1)$ 的 ReLU 网络中，$L^2$ 逼近误差的下确界不可达；即深层 ReLU 网络一般不满足最佳逼近性质，即使目标函数连续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层 ReLU 网络在典型回归任务中存在全局极小值（最佳逼近性质），但深层网络是否保持该性质尚不明确，作者旨在检验深层情形下最佳逼近性质是否普遍成立。

Method: 在 $[-1,1]^2$ 上显式构造一个连续函数作为反例，对架构为 $(2,2,1,1)$（两个隐藏层）的 ReLU 网络类研究其 $L^2$ 逼近误差下确界；证明关键在于第二隐藏层神经元的折线（breakline）可以弯曲，从而破坏单隐藏层情形所依赖的对称性论证。

Result: 所构造的连续函数使得该网络类上的 $L^2$ 逼近误差下确界无法被任何网络参数达到，表明深层 ReLU 网络缺乏最佳逼近性质。

Conclusion: 深层 ReLU 网络一般不满足最佳逼近性质，这与浅层网络的结果形成鲜明对比；折线可弯曲性是该现象的核心机制。

Abstract: We construct a continuous function on $[-1,1]^2$ for which the infimum of the $L^2$-approximation error over the class of ReLU networks with two hidden layers and architecture $(2,2,1,1)$ is not attained. This counterexample shows that deep ReLU networks do not satisfy the best approximation property in general, even for continuous target functions. Our findings contrast with existing results for shallow neural networks, where ReLU is one of the few activation functions that guarantee the existence of global minima in typical regression tasks. The proof is based on the fact that the breaklines for neurons in the second hidden layer can bend, which breaks the symmetry arguments used in the one-hidden-layer case.

</details>


### [20] [MoEZip: Routing-Aware KV Cache Compression for Sparse Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30206)
*Minsang Kim,Seungjun Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoEZip：面向稀疏MoE语言模型的、基于路由敏感性的KV cache压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型长上下文推理中KV cache随序列长度线性增长，成为主要内存瓶颈；现有KV压缩方法针对稠密Transformer，忽略了MoE特有问题——KV驱逐会改变答案生成时的专家选择，从而损害生成质量。

Method: 提出基于路由分布Fisher信息矩阵的新度量，估计专家路由对扰动的敏感性；将该度量与注意力权重结合，对上下文进行路由感知打分；并设计面向稠密结构与稀疏MoE结构的互补评分/驱逐系统组合。

Result: 在多项基准上，激进压缩下表现稳健；KV保留率10%时，LongBench与RULER上平均较SotA基线提升+24与+20分，对应分数分别提升2.3倍与4.9倍。

Conclusion: 路由感知的KV cache压缩能有效缓解MoE长上下文推理的内存瓶颈，在极限压缩比下显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models scale model capacity efficiently by activating a small subset of parameters per token. However, in long-context inference, memory remains a major bottleneck because the KV cache grows linearly with sequence length. Existing KV cache compression methods reduce this cost for dense transformers, but overlook a problem specific to MoE: KV eviction can change which experts are selected during answer generation, degrading the generation quality. We propose MoEZip, a routing-aware KV cache compression for sparse MoE language models. To estimate the sensitivity of expert routing to perturbation, we propose a novel metric based on the Fisher information matrix of routing distributions. MoEZip combines this metric with attention weights for routing-aware scoring of contexts. Finally, we compose a complementary scoring system that combines eviction methods for dense and sparse-MoE architectures. Experiments show that MoEZip achieves robust performance under aggressive cache compression on various benchmarks. At a KV retention ratio of 10\%, MoEZip improves over the SoTA baseline by +24 and +20 points on average on LongBench and RULER, corresponding to 2.3$\times$ and 4.9$\times$ higher scores, respectively.

</details>


### [21] [Spectral Uncertainty Decomposition for Sparse Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30207)
*Ismail Huseynov,Stefan Haufe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于先验预条件前向算子奇异值结构的谱框架，将M/EEG源成像后验协方差精确分解为数据信息分量与不可约不确定性分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M/EEG分布式源估计是病态逆问题，不确定性量化困难；常规条件高斯后验协方差无法区分似然可见方向上的不确定性与测量不可见的不确定性。

Method: 基于先验预条件前向算子的奇异值结构构建谱分解，将后验协方差分解为数据信息与不确定性（indeterminacy）认知分量；并推导等价的减法分解，把先验协方差表示为后验项与数据驱动缩减项之和。

Result: 给出后验协方差的精确、可解释分解，可识别可通过更好测量降低的不确定性成分与由传感几何决定的根本不可约成分，并实现贝叶斯学习带来的不确定性缩减的精确核算。

Conclusion: 两种互补表示在线性高斯贝叶斯逆求解器（后验协方差具闭式解）下，为M/EEG源成像提供兼具理论洞见与实际价值的可解释不确定性量化工具。

Abstract: Estimating distributed neural sources from M/EEG measurements is an ill-posed inverse problem where principled uncertainty quantification remains challenging. The usual conditional Gaussian posterior covariance does not explicitly distinguish uncertainty along likelihood-visible source directions from uncertainty that is invisible to the measurements. We propose a spectral framework based on the singular value structure of the prior-preconditioned forward operator, which provides an exact, interpretable decomposition of the posterior covariance into data-informed and indeterminacy epistemic components. This formulation identifies uncertainty contributions that are reducible by better measurements from those that are fundamentally irreducible by sensing geometry. We further derive an equivalent subtractive decomposition that expresses the prior covariance as the sum of posterior and data-driven reduction terms, enabling precise accounting of uncertainty reduction through Bayesian learning. Together, these complementary representations offer both theoretical insight and practical tools for interpretable uncertainty quantification in M/EEG source imaging for linear-Gaussian Bayesian inverse solvers where the posterior covariance is available in closed form.

</details>


### [22] [Prediction Is Not Calibration: Item Parameter Recovery and Representation Coupling in Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30211)
*Wenrui Yuan,Stéphanie M. van den Berg,Johannes Steinrücke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 知识追踪模型的预测准确率不能保证题目参数可恢复；区分度与选项斜率受能力估计尺度影响，需将题目表示宽度与参数键宽度解耦并单独评估参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KT模型仅以下一响应预测训练题目参数解码器，但高预测准确率不保证题目参数（如区分度、选项斜率）被正确恢复，二者评价目标存在错位，需厘清参数不可恢复的原因并给出改进设计。

Method: 将2PL、GPCM、NRM解码器与LSTM、Transformer、DKVMN编码器组合；通过响应模型导数分析区分度与选项斜率更新对能力估计尺度与散布的依赖；用仿射logit恒等式说明能力估计中题目相关的平移与缩放可被题目参数吸收而不改变响应概率；仿真中比较表示宽度、乱序/固定顺序，并将序列题目表示宽度d_S与题目参数键宽度d_M分离（如SK(64,8)），与共享宽度基线SH(64)对比；在TIMSS与EdNet上与离线IRT参考比较。

Result: 仿真中难度、平均阈值与正确选项截距的恢复在打乱顺序、不同表示宽度下均保持较高；区分度与正确选项斜率对比的恢复需更宽的题目表示，且在固定顺序下进一步退化。SK(64,8)减轻固定顺序下的能力扭曲，提升恢复与跨顺序一致性，且不损失测试准确率、模型规模与SH(64)相当。TIMSS与EdNet上增宽d_M一致提升与离线IRT参考的一致性，收窄d_S的代价取决于编码器对序列宽度的依赖。

Conclusion: 题目参数（尤其区分度与选项斜率）必须与响应预测分开评估；解耦题目参数键宽度与序列表示宽度是提升参数可恢复性与跨顺序稳健性的有效设计。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) models can estimate item parameters through an item response decoder trained solely for next-response prediction, yet predictive accuracy does not guarantee parameter recovery. We study 2PL, GPCM, and NRM decoders paired with LSTM, Transformer, and DKVMN encoders. Response-model derivatives show why discrimination and option slope updates depend directly on the scale and spread of estimated ability. Affine logit identities show how item-dependent shifts and rescaling in estimated ability are absorbed into item parameters without changing response probabilities. In simulations, difficulty, mean threshold, and correct-option intercept recovery remain high under shuffled order across representation widths, whereas discrimination and keyed option slope contrast recovery require wider item representations and degrade further under fixed item order. Separating the sequence item representation (width $d_S$) from the item parameter key (width $d_M$), and pairing a wide key with a thin sequence representation ($\mathrm{SK}(64,8)$), reduces fixed-order ability distortions and improves recovery and cross-order consistency without sacrificing test accuracy or increasing model size relative to a shared width-64 baseline ($\mathrm{SH}(64)$). On TIMSS and EdNet, widening $d_M$ consistently improves agreement with an offline IRT reference, while the trade-off from narrowing $d_S$ depends on the encoder's reliance on sequence width for prediction. These findings show why item parameters, especially discrimination and option slopes, must be evaluated separately from response prediction.

</details>


### [23] [AI Mindmeld: A Latent Interconnect Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30215)
*Siavash Golkar,Jake Kovalic,Shirley Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latent Interconnect Protocol (LIP)，训练轻量连接器将领域专家模型的内部表征直接映射为LLM内部状态，实现跨模型表征直连（mindmeld），在冻结全部预训练模型的前提下支持零样本跨域任务与组合式专家扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为科学智能体需调用专家工具，但专家信息只能以文本/图像/音频/视频进入LLM上下文，构成高维表征与LLM之间的瓶颈，且会丢失LLM"想不到去请求"的隐含属性。

Method: LIP学习连接器，把专家表征映射进LLM内部状态；仅训练连接器、冻结所有预训练模型。接入3个蛋白质和3个小分子专家到Gemma 3（4B/12B）与Kimi K2.6（总1T、激活32B）；连接器独立训练，不改动LLM及已有连接器，支持组合。

Result: 独立训练的连接器可在不改变LLM与既有连接器的情况下提供有用信息，支持对未见对象的熟悉与新型问题；实现无联合训练的零样本跨域任务迁移；任务性能与组合性总体随LLM能力提升，零样本迁移增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 表征直连可行且具有可扩展性与组合性，但当前LLM未针对专家表征原生输入训练，访问不等于可靠解读、整合与推理使用；呼吁专家方提供一致表征接口，LLM方训练模型接收、融合并推理此类输入。代码与预训练连接器已开源。

Abstract: LLMs can act as scientific agents using tools to consult specialists and run experiments. Yet specialist information must enter their context, generally as text, images, audio or video: a bottleneck between models with rich, high-dimensional representations. We advocate direct representation exchange: a mindmeld conveying many properties, including information an LLM would not think to request. It should be extensible to new specialists, flexible across questions and tasks, and compositional across independently connected specialists without joint training.
  The Latent Interconnect Protocol (LIP) learns connectors mapping specialist representations into LLM internal states. Training only connectors keeps this practical as LLMs grow and evolve. We connect three protein and three small molecule specialists to Gemma 3 (4B and 12B parameters) and Kimi K2.6 (1T total, 32B active), freezing all pretrained models. Independently trained connectors provide useful information without changing the LLM or existing connectors, supporting familiar and new question types about unseen objects. We demonstrate zero-shot transfer to cross-domain tasks without joint training. Task performance and compositionality generally improve with LLM capability, with especially strong gains in zero-shot task transfer. Limitations remain: current LLMs were not trained for specialist representations as native inputs; access does not ensure reliable interpretation, integration or use in reasoning. We call on specialist developers to provide consistent representation interfaces and, especially, on LLM developers to train models to receive, combine and reason with these inputs. These efforts could expand scientific agents' information while preserving conventional tools' flexibility.
  Data, pretrained connectors, and code for training, evaluation and interactive inference: https://github.com/PolymathicAI/AI-Mindmeld

</details>


### [24] [When Reconstruction Is Not Enough: Doubly Robust Counterfactual and Interventional Mediation Analysis with Learned Multimodal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30220)
*Cong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究多模态健康记录的表示学习用于因果推断时的充分性问题：证明任何仅基于协变量的训练目标都无法保证表示足以支持识别，提出表征偏差的一阶分解及一套以模态特异VAE、交叉拟合双稳健估计、锁定表型与诊断工具组成的流程，模拟中缺失罕见混杂导致间接效应区间覆盖率仅2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多模态健康记录的表示被广泛用于混杂调整，但训练目标通常是重建数据而非保留因果识别所需信息；在替代治疗下的反事实结局（表型特异效应、数字孪生式个体反事实风险谱）以及暴露诱导中间混杂下的干预性直接/间接效应中，表示的因果充分性缺乏保证与检验手段。

Method: 给出表示保持可识别性的条件，比较反事实对照与干预性中介的识别要求；证明仅基于协变量的训练目标（重建、对比、物理约束）均无法认证表示的充分性，并在线性结构模型中构造重建精度损失任意小却偏差任意大的反例；将交叉拟合双稳健估计量的误差分解为一阶表征偏差与二阶 nuisance 估计余项；提出工作流：在估计样本外训练模态特异变分自编码器、交叉拟合双稳健估计、锁定表型、select-then-confirm 通路分析、可移植表示，以及表示不充分、暴露泄漏与敏感性诊断。

Result: 模拟中丢弃一个罕见但中等强度的混杂因子，使 n=3,000 时间接效应置信区间覆盖率降至约2%；重建损失未检出该问题，而一个简单的预测性诊断在所有重复中均成功标记。

Conclusion: 表示的因果充分性不能由协变量仅训练目标保证，且双稳健性与交叉拟合不能消除一阶表征偏差，样本量增大时覆盖率反而恶化；报告因果估计时须配套表示诊断、样本外训练与锁定表型等流程性保障。

Abstract: Learned representations of high-dimensional multimodal health records are increasingly used to adjust for confounding, yet they are usually trained to reconstruct the data rather than preserve the information that causal identification requires. We study counterfactual outcomes under alternative treatments, including phenotype-specific effects and patient-level counterfactual risk profiles of the kind digital twins provide, and interventional direct and indirect effects under exposure-induced intermediate confounding. We give conditions under which a representation preserves identification and show that interventional mediation is more demanding than counterfactual comparison. We prove that no covariate-only training objective, whether reconstruction, contrastive, or physics-informed, can certify that a representation is sufficient, and that, in a linear structural model, a representation can lose arbitrarily little reconstruction accuracy yet induce arbitrarily large bias. We decompose the error of cross-fitted doubly robust estimators into a first-order representation bias, which neither double robustness nor cross-fitting removes, and a second-order remainder of nuisance-estimation errors, so coverage worsens as the sample size grows when the representation is insufficient. We propose a workflow combining modality-specific variational autoencoders trained outside the estimation sample, cross-fitted doubly robust estimators, locked phenotypes, select-then-confirm pathway analysis, portable representations, and diagnostics for representation insufficiency, exposure leakage, and sensitivity. In simulations, discarding a rare, moderately strong confounder gave indirect-effect intervals with 2% coverage at n = 3,000; reconstruction loss missed the problem, and a simple predictive diagnostic flagged it in every replicate.

</details>


### [25] [How Prompt Style Affects AI Accuracy and Speed in Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30223)
*Saad B. Alarifi,Mohammed A. Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在GPT-5.5上对9种提示风格各取1个提示、覆盖IOI的3个正确性任务与2个NP-hard速度任务进行探索性研究，发现不存在通用最优提示：最强提示取决于任务类型以及结果按均值还是中位数汇总，且许多均值差异由少数离群运行驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅在正确性（accuracy）层面比较竞赛编程的提示策略，未考察运行时间/速度，也未将结果与人类选手表现进行对照，因此对提示风格影响的认识不完整。

Method: 探索性实验：9种提示风格，每种取1个提示；模型为GPT-5.5；任务包括国际信息学奥林匹克（IOI）的3个正确性任务和2个NP-hard速度任务；分别以均值与中位数汇总结果；并与人类IOI选手作描述性对比（注意实验条件不同）。

Result: 无单一最佳提示；最强提示随任务类型和汇总统计量（均值/中位数）变化；均值分数的多数差异源于少数离群运行；相对人类IOI选手，AI并非严格更优或更差——在1个任务上超过平均选手，在另2个任务上不及。

Conclusion: 提示风格应与优化目标匹配而非视为通用方案；单一指标评估会掩盖提示有效性的波动；将提示比较从正确性扩展到运行速度并与人类表现对齐，可给出提示风格对竞赛编程表现影响的更全面图景。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in competitive programming, and their outputs are sensitive to how a prompt is phrased. Prior work has compared prompt strategies for accuracy on competitive programming, but not for runtime speed, and has not situated the results against human performance. In an exploratory study of one prompt in each of nine styles on GPT-5.5 across three accuracy tasks from the International Olympiad in Informatics (IOI) and two NP-hard speed tasks, we found that no single prompt was best: the strongest prompt depended on the task type and on whether results were summarized by the mean or the median, suggesting that, at least for these tasks, prompt style should be matched to the objective rather than treated as universal. Many differences in mean scores came from a few outlier runs, showing that evaluating prompting by a single metric can obscure how much its effectiveness varies. Compared descriptively with human IOI contestants, who worked under different conditions, the AI was neither strictly better nor strictly worse, exceeding the average contestant on one task but falling short on the other two. By extending prompt comparison from accuracy to runtime speed and grounding it against human performance, this study gives a broader picture of how prompt style affects competitive programming performance.

</details>


### [26] [GANet: A Lightweight Group Attention Network for Plant Disease Identification on Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30225)
*Anubhab Tripathi,Bibek Das,Asfak Ali,Sheli Shinha Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轻量级的组等变注意力网络GANet，用于38种植物病害的预测与监测，仅0.001M参数即在PlantVillage上达到99.99%准确率，并可在树莓派4b与Jetson Nano上分别以5 FPS与11 FPS实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球变暖与气候变化加剧植物病害、威胁粮食生产，需持续监测与早期检测；但现有深度学习模型算力需求高、难以在低端硬件高效运行，且多局限于特定作物病害，通用性不足。

Method: 提出轻量级Group Equivariance Attention Network（GANet），引入新颖的Group Attention模块与Group-Wise Feature Fusion模块，使模型兼具稀疏性与高效性；并针对Raspberry Pi 4b、Jetson Nano等边缘设备进行部署优化。

Result: 仅0.001M参数，在PlantVillage数据集上取得99.99%准确率；在Raspberry Pi 4b与Jetson Nano上分别达到5 FPS和11 FPS。

Conclusion: GANet以极低参数量实现高精度多类别（38种）植物病害识别，并具备在低功耗边缘设备上实时监测的潜力，为资源受限场景下的植物病害监测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Global warming and climate change are major catalysts for plant diseases, significantly affecting food production worldwide. Addressing this issue requires continuous monitoring and early detection of plant diseases. While recent advancements in deep learning offer potential solutions, most models demand high processing power and struggle to perform efficiently on low-end electronic hardware. Furthermore, many existing works focus on specific crop diseases, limiting their broader applicability. This paper presents a lightweight Group Equivariance Attention Network (GANet) designed for the prediction and monitoring of 38 types of plant diseases. GANet introduces a novel Group Attention block and Group-Wise Feature Fusion block, making it both sparse and efficient. With only 0.001M parameters, the model achieves an impressive 99.99% accuracy on the PlantVillage dataset. Additionally, GANet is optimized for deployment on edge devices such as the Raspberry Pi 4b and Jetson Nano, where it reaches 5 FPS and 11 FPS, respectively, demonstrating its potential for real-time plant disease monitoring on low-power devices.

</details>


### [27] [Benchmarking Model Compression Techniques Across Heterogeneous Edge Platforms: A Predictive Maintenance Use Case for Rotating Machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30228)
*Matheus Calheira Guimarães de Oliveira,Marco Aurélio da Silva Cruz,Regis Cardoso,Lucas Cambuim,Rafael Macieira,Arthur Paim,Helaine Neves,Alex Santos,Lilian Guarieiro,Jean Schmith,Prashant Kumar,Erick G. Sperandio Nascimento*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文针对工业边缘设备上深度学习模型部署难题，系统比较四种模型压缩技术并跨九种异构计算平台评测，发现知识蒸馏在预测性维护任务上综合最优：超过90%推理加速、91.22% balanced F-score、98.9%模型体积缩减且性能几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向Industry 5.0，需将复杂深度学习模型高效部署到资源受限的边缘硬件；但工业边缘环境硬件异构性强、模型压缩易导致性能退化，且缺乏针对预测性维护等应用场景的压缩技术选型指导。

Method: 以旋转机械真实数据集上的高性能CNN预测性维护模型为对象，采用四种先进模型压缩技术进行处理，并在九种异构计算平台上（从高度受限的边缘设备到通用及高性能处理器）进行部署评测，联合考察分类性能、推理速度与压缩比。

Result: 知识蒸馏为最有效方案：所有设备上推理加速超过90%，维持91.22%的balanced F-score，相较未压缩模型预测性能下降极小，模型尺寸缩减98.9%。

Conclusion: 通过在多种边缘平台的成功部署与系统性压缩策略对比，验证了在不牺牲预测性能的前提下大幅降低计算成本与能耗的可行路径，支撑Industry 5.0目标实现。

Abstract: To enable Industry 5.0, where autonomous systems are widely adopted and integrated with people to promote sustainable development in industries, it is imperative to adapt complex deep learning models to operate efficiently on resource-constrained hardware for edge inference. However, adapting these models remains challenging due to the hardware heterogeneity of edge computing in industrial settings, performance degradation caused by model compression, and the lack of clear guidance on which compression techniques are practical and effective for applications such as predictive maintenance. To address these challenges, this paper presents a comprehensive comparative analysis of four state-of-the-art model compression techniques applied to a high-performance convolutional neural network for predictive maintenance using a real-world dataset of rotating machinery, equipment typically used in industry. The compressed models were evaluated across a heterogeneous set of nine computing platforms, ranging from highly constrained edge computing devices to general purpose and high-performance processors, reflecting the diversity and fragmentation typical of industrial edge environments. By jointly prioritizing classification performance, inference speed, and compression ratio, we found that compression using knowledge distillation emerged as the most effective approach. The model had more than 90% speedup across all devices while maintaining a 91.22% balanced F-score, with a very small drop in predictive performance relative to the uncompressed model, along with a 98.9% size reduction. Through successful deployment across multiple edge platforms and a systematic comparison of compression strategies, we demonstrated a clear path to achieving Industry 5.0 targets, drastically cutting computational costs and, consequently, energy consumption without sacrificing predictive performance.

</details>


### [28] [Automated screening of privacy risks in medical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30229)
*Konstantinos Vrettos,Eleftherios Tzanis,Haridimos Kondylakis,Evangelia E. Vassalou,Florence X. Doo,Michail E. Klontzas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统测量了7,789个医学AI模型在6个临床数据集（4种模态）上的成员推断攻击（MIA）患者级脆弱性，发现风险最高的患者在训练配置变化下高度稳定，并提出一种用四个小模型提前筛查高危患者记录的轻量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学AI模型常在无训练数据的情况下被共享，通常假设这样可保护贡献数据的患者；但模型会泄露某患者记录是否用于训练（成员推断攻击）。该风险通常以队列平均值报告，看似可忽略，却可能掩盖个体患者几乎必然被暴露的问题。作者希望在生产模型训练前识别可能脆弱的记录，使隐私评估更实用。

Method: 对每位患者测量其对成员推断攻击的脆弱性，覆盖6个临床数据集、4种模态、共7,789个模型，包括两个适配的预训练医学模型；比较不同训练选择（如权重衰减、架构、初始化、基础模型）对高危患者人数与排名的影 响，并基于风险排名的稳定性开发一个使用四个小模型的轻量筛查工具；同时评估被标记患者能否在不移除其数据的前提下得到保护。

Result: 训练选择会改变高危攻击分数患者的人数；更强的权重衰减可将暴露队列缩小最多三倍。然而在测试的训练配方、架构与初始化（包括基础模型）之间，风险排名最高的患者基本保持一致。基于此稳定性，四小模型筛查工具可在生产模型训练前识别高危暴露患者；并评估了不移除数据情况下保护被标记患者的可能性。

Conclusion: 患者级MIA脆弱性具有跨配置的稳定性，使得低成本预筛查成为可行；隐私筛查应纳入临床AI的数据准备流程。

Abstract: Medical artificial intelligence (AI) models are often shared without their training data, assuming this protects the patients who contributed them. However, a model can reveal whether a patient's records were used in training, a privacy breach known as a membership inference attack. This risk is usually reported as a cohort average, which can appear negligible while individual patients are almost certainly exposed. Identifying potentially vulnerable records before developing a production model could make privacy assessment more practical. We measured each patient's vulnerability to membership inference attack, across 7,789 models trained on six clinical datasets in four modalities, including two adapted pretrained medical models. Training choices changed how many patients had high attack scores, but the patients who ranked highest by risk largely stayed the same across the configurations we tested. Stronger weight decay reduced the exposed cohort up to threefold, yet largely the same patients remained most at risk across training recipes, architectures and initializations, including foundation models. Therefore, we developed a lightweight screening tool that uses four small models to identify patients with high risk of exposure before production-model training. We also assessed if flagged patients could be protected without removing their data. Privacy screening should become incorporated into data preparation for clinical AI.

</details>


### [29] [Edit, Don't Imitate: Minimal-Edit On-Policy Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30231)
*Wen Ye,Ailin Deng,Zirui Wang,Shixing Yu,Han Zhang,Tian Yun,Nikhil Mehta,Tarun Kalluri,Xuefei Cao,Xuezhe Ma,Hengduo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“最小编辑纠正式同策略蒸馏”（minimal-edit corrective on-policy distillation）：仅在训练时使用参考图描述与已验证参考解，让教师以最小编辑目标对错误的学生 rollout 打分，仅对教师不认可的 token 计入纠正损失；正确 rollout 用任务奖励 RL 训练。推理时无需图描述即可达到甚至超过“base 模型+推理期参考描述”的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉数学推理的瓶颈常在推理开始前：VLM 误读图形后，后续推理建立在错误前提上。小模型尤为明显，而准确的文本描述能显著缩小与大模型的差距。但推理期获取这类描述需额外标注或模型调用，成本高，故希望在训练中内化该信息。

Method: 1）训练期信息：参考图形描述 + 已验证参考解，测试期不使用。2）教师以最小编辑目标为错误学生 rollout 打分，定位需改动的 token。3）仅教师不认可的 token 贡献纠正损失，避免整段响应模仿，保持同策略监督。4）正确 rollout 采用任务奖励强化学习。5）教师无需自行生成解，只需评估纠正，因此同尺寸冻结教师亦有效。

Result: 跨模型规模上，测试时无需描述即可达到或超过“对应 base 模型 + 推理期参考图描述”。感知类任务（尤其低级几何感知）性能提升，并可迁移到外部数学与感知基准。同尺寸冻结教师仍有效，但多数指标上增益小于更大教师。

Conclusion: 该方法以训练期最小编辑式纠正监督替代推理期描述依赖，在保持同策略性的同时避免全响应模仿，兼顾感知与推理能力，具备跨规模与跨基准的可迁移性；教师规模影响增益幅度。

Abstract: Visual mathematical reasoning can fail before reasoning begins: when a vision-language model (VLM) misreads a diagram, subsequent reasoning proceeds from false premises. This perceptual bottleneck is especially pronounced in smaller models, while accurate textual descriptions can substantially narrow the gap to larger models. Because obtaining such descriptions at inference requires additional annotation or model calls, we propose minimal-edit corrective on-policy distillation. Using reference figure descriptions and verified reference solutions as training-only information, a teacher scores incorrect student rollouts under a minimal-edit objective, and only teacher-disfavored tokens contribute to the corrective loss. Correct rollouts receive task-reward reinforcement learning. This preserves on-policy supervision while avoiding full-response imitation. Across model scales, the method requires no descriptions at test time and meets or exceeds the corresponding base model supplied with a reference figure description at inference. It also improves perception-oriented performance, particularly low-level geometric perception, and transfers to external mathematics and perception benchmarks. Because the minimal-edit teacher evaluates corrections rather than generating its own solution, the method remains effective with a frozen same-size teacher, although its gains are smaller than with a larger teacher on most metrics.

</details>


### [30] [A Quadratic Lens on Muon's Polar Update: Benefits, Pitfalls, and the Role of Newton-Schulz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30280)
*Egor Shulgin,Sam Laing,Antonio Orvieto,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文以矩阵二次型为可解析模型研究Muon核心的正交化更新PolarGD，在线搜索下比较更新方向，揭示正交化何时以及为何有助优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon沿近似正交化方向更新矩阵参数，常比AdamW更快训练大语言模型，但正交化何时有效、为何有效的理论机制尚不清晰。

Method: 在矩阵二次型上分析PolarGD，使用闭式解，并在线搜索下比较PolarGD、梯度下降与sign descent的更新方向，剔除学习率调参的影响；同时分析Muon中有限步Newton-Schulz迭代的作用。

Result: 正交化在梯度远离低秩且最大奇异方向曲率较高时收益显著；当误差行正交且等范数时PolarGD对任意Hessian一步收敛，而梯度下降需所有Hessian特征值相等；PolarGD对问题旋转不变，sign descent则不然。多步下PolarGD相当于以当前迭代点为预条件的梯度下降，速度由迭代点的条件数而非Hessian条件数决定，这既解释了快收敛区，也解释了迭代点含极小奇异值时的慢收敛区。有限次Newton-Schulz迭代像梯度下降一样处理这些极小奇异值，故迭代更少反而可能收敛更快；在Muon训练的transformer中，注意力矩阵约四分之一的奇异值落入该区间。

Conclusion: PolarGD/Muon的优势源于沿近似正交方向更新，其速率由迭代点条件数控制；精确正交化并非总是最优，有限步Newton-Schulz在极小奇异值情形下可带来更快收敛。

Abstract: Muon updates matrix-valued parameters along approximately orthogonalized directions and often trains large language models faster than AdamW, yet it remains unclear when and why orthogonalization helps. We study PolarGD, the orthogonalized update at the core of Muon, on matrix quadratics, where optimizers can be analyzed in closed form, and compare update directions under line search to remove the effect of learning-rate tuning. Orthogonalization pays off when the gradient is far from low rank and its largest singular directions see high curvature. PolarGD reaches the minimizer in one step whenever the rows of the error are orthogonal with equal norms, for any Hessian, whereas gradient descent needs all Hessian eigenvalues to be equal; and unlike sign descent, PolarGD is unaffected by rotations of the problem. Over many steps, PolarGD acts as gradient descent preconditioned by the current iterate, so its speed is governed by the conditioning of the iterate rather than of the Hessian. This explains fast regimes as well as a slow one, caused by tiny singular values of the iterate, in which PolarGD is much slower than gradient descent. Finally, the few Newton-Schulz iterations used in Muon treat these tiny singular values as gradient descent does, so fewer iterations can converge faster than exact orthogonalization; in a transformer trained with Muon, about a quarter of the singular values of the attention matrices fall in this regime.

</details>


### [31] [SWE-VERIFIER: Selective Verification for Cost-Efficient Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30284)
*Kaijian Zou,Vaibhav Chennareddy,Yunxiang Zhang,Naihao Deng,Longju Bai,Jiaxin Pei,Rada Mihalcea,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWE-Verifier 是一个轻量生成模型，选择性地触发候选动作采样与验证，在低于 4% 的步骤上做验证，即可在低成本（约 1.1 倍）下提升编码智能体的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体大量算力消耗在动作生成与长交互历史处理上；劣质动作会把智能体引入低价值状态，浪费算力并抬高 API 成本。采样并验证替代动作虽能改善决策，但每步都做会带来巨大开销，因此需要"何时验证"的选择机制。

Method: 提出 SWE-Verifier：基于 Qwen3.5-4B 训练的轻量生成式模型。先对编码智能体提出的动作打分以判断是否需要额外候选采样，再对候选动作排序后执行。训练数据为偏好数据，将由环境观测对比的对比动作与评分标准（rubric）引导的教师评分分组构造。

Result: 在 mini-swe-agent 框架下，MiniMax-M2.7 上解决率由 75.47% 提升至 77.20%，成本为 1.09 倍；在未训练其动作的 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 上由 72.60% 提升至 74.27%，成本 1.13 倍。两模型上验证步骤均少于 4%，且以远低成本的代价达到与全轨迹基线、全步验证基线相当或更高的解决率。

Conclusion: 选择性候选采样与轻量验证在编码智能体的性能与推理效率上具有互补收益；模型、代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Coding agents spend substantial computation generating actions and processing long interaction histories. Poor action choices can steer agents into low-value states, wasting computation and increasing API costs. Sampling and verifying alternative actions can improve the agent's decisions, but applying this procedure at every step adds substantial overhead. We introduce SWE-Verifier, a lightweight generative model that selectively triggers candidate sampling and verification. It scores the coding agent's proposed action to determine when additional candidate sampling is needed, then ranks the resulting actions before execution. We train SWE-Verifier from Qwen3.5-4B using preference data that groups contrasting actions with their resulting environment observations and rubric-guided teacher scores. Using the mini-swe-agent harness, SWE-Verifier improves resolution from 75.47% to 77.20% on MiniMax-M2.7 at 1.09 times cost and generalizes to DeepSeek-V4-Flash without training on its actions, improving resolution from 72.60% to 74.27% at 1.13 times cost. Across both models, SWE-Verifier verifies fewer than 4% of steps and achieves comparable or higher resolution than the trajectory and all-step baselines at substantially lower cost. These results demonstrate that selective candidate sampling and lightweight verification provide complementary gains in coding-agent performance and inference efficiency. Our model, code, and data are available at https://github.com/zkjzou/SWE-Verifier.

</details>


### [32] [Generalizing Metropolis-Hastings for DAG Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30291)
*Daniele Nikzad,Mikko Koivisto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"二重采样"(duet sampling)——Metropolis-Hastings 的推广，每次迭代从提案池中选取两个分布分别用于生成候选状态与评估返回概率，并据此构造可同时重采样多个节点父集的 MCMC 移动，从而更高效地近似 DAG 后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯网络结构学习的核心难题是高效近似有向无环图(DAG)的后验分布；现有方法在大型高维数据上仍不可行，已有 MCMC 移动的提案池受限，混合效率不足。

Method: 提出可同时重采样多个节点父集的 MCMC 新移动；其核心为 duet sampling：每步从提案分布池中选两个分布，一个生成候选状态、另一个用于评估回到当前状态的概率，二者共同构成接受准则；配对不同提案分布在不增加计算开销的前提下扩大自由度、提升链的混合效率。

Result: 实验表明新移动单独使用及组合使用均优于依赖更受限移动池的先前最优方法；即使让基线方法也使用 duet sampling 以增强其性能，该优势仍然保持。

Conclusion: Duet sampling 是一种通用且无额外计算成本的 MCMC 扩展，多节点联合重采样移动可显著提升 DAG 后验推断的效率，并为现有方法提供可即插即用的改进。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in Bayesian network structure learning lies in efficiently approximating the posterior distribution of directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). For large, high-dimensional datasets, the task remains beyond the capabilities of current methods. This work advances Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithms by introducing novel moves that simultaneously resample the parents for multiple nodes of the DAG. At the core of these moves is duet sampling, a new generalization of the Metropolis-Hastings algorithm that selects two proposal distributions from a pool at each iteration. One is used for generating a candidate state of the Markov chain and another for evaluating the return to the current state; both are ingredients of an appropriately crafted acceptance criterion. Pairing different proposal distributions provides additional freedom and enables constructing Markov chains that mix more efficiently than their basic Metropolis-Hastings counterparts, at no additional computational cost. Experiments show that our new moves, both individually and in combination, outperform the previous state of the art, which relies on a more restrictive pool of moves. This advantage persists even when letting the previous methods leverage duet sampling to enhance their performance.

</details>


### [33] [GAV: A scientific experimentation environment for PDE agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30292)
*Muheng Li,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAV（Generator-Agent-Verifier）科学实验环境，利用制造解方法让新PDE问题"构造即可验证"，无需昂贵参考计算，实现Agent程序迭代改进与可靠性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体开发PDE程序依赖可靠反馈，但在新PDE问题上评估需昂贵参考计算，固定数据集又限制了实验范围。

Method: Generator用数值模拟或实验测量数据学习连续参考解分布以表征目标物理区域；借助制造解方法（MMS）从生成参考解反推构造匹配的PDE问题，避免单独数值求解；Agent设计实验、分析失败并迭代改进程序；Verifier对候选程序与参考解比对，给出精度与计算代价的数值反馈，并据此生成探测程序弱点的针对性新问题；同时对评估可靠性与引导式开发进行理论分析。

Result: 在Navier-Stokes与Vlasov-Poisson类方程、不同数据来源与物理区域的实验中验证有效性；数值Navier-Stokes任务上，相比原始设置，GAV将三种现有PDE智能体的平均测试误差降低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: GAV通过构造式可验证问题生成与数值反馈闭环，为PDE程序开发提供可扩展、低成本且可靠的评测与引导机制，显著提升现有PDE智能体的性能。

Abstract: Reliable feedback is essential for coding agents to develop programs for partial differential equations (PDEs). Yet evaluating these programs on new PDE problems often requires expensive reference computations, while fixed datasets restrict the range of experiments. We introduce Generator-Agent-Verifier (GAV), a scientific experimentation environment that makes new PDE problems verifiable by construction. The Generator learns a distribution of continuous reference solutions using data from numerical simulations or experimental measurements to represent a target physical regime. Using the method of manufactured solutions, it works backward from the generated reference solutions to construct matching PDE problems without a separate numerical solve. The Agent designs experiments, investigates failures, and iteratively improves its program. The Verifier evaluates candidate programs against the reference solutions and provides numerical feedback on accuracy and computational cost. This feedback can also guide the generation of new problems that probe the current program's weaknesses. We provide a theoretical analysis of evaluation reliability and guided development. Experiments on Navier-Stokes and Vlasov-Poisson-type equations across different data sources and regimes demonstrate the effectiveness of GAV. In particular, on the numerical Navier-Stokes task, GAV reduces the mean test error of three existing PDE agents by more than an order of magnitude relative to their original setups. Code is available at https://github.com/PotatoJnny/PDE-GAV.

</details>


### [34] [Spike-Aware Gradient-Based Mixed-Precision Quantization of Spiking Neural Networks for Vision Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30339)
*Rajshekhar Rakshit,Devgokul B.,Rakshitha Kalkura,Gopalakrishnan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种脉冲感知的、基于一阶梯度的 SNN 混合精度量化框架，用梯度信息自适应分配位宽，避免二阶 Hessian 计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN 因事件驱动与稀疏计算而具能效优势，但实际部署所需的低精度条件下如何保持精度尚少研究；现有基于 Hessian 的量化方法（如 HAWQ-V2）在多时间步展开下带来显著内存与计算开销。

Method: 以脉冲感知的一阶梯度信息进行混合精度位宽分配，取代昂贵的二阶 Hessian 计算；在 VGG、ResNet 与 Vision Transformer 上，覆盖 ANN-to-SNN 转换与直接训练两类框架，于 CIFAR10/100、ImageNet、DVS-CIFAR-10 上评估，并给出混合精度 SNN 的能耗分析。

Result: 在 ImageNet 上的 ResNet-34 上，4.78× 压缩下达到 73.30% 精度，与 HAWQ-V2 相当，同时内存降低 2.31×、位宽分配运行时间加速 100×。

Conclusion: 所提一阶梯度混合精度量化策略可在不损失性能的前提下实现 SNN 的能效部署，并显著降低量化搜索的内存与时间成本。

Abstract: In recent years, Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have gained massive attention as an energy-efficient neural computing paradigm due to their event-driven and sparse computing capability. However, maintaining accuracy under low-precision constraints, necessary for practical deployment, has been largely unexplored for SNN models. In addition, existing Hessian-based approaches for quantization, such as HAWQ-V2, incur significant memory and computational overhead due to temporal unrolling across multiple SNN timesteps. We propose a spike-aware gradient-based mixed-precision quantization framework for SNNs that adaptively assigns bit-widths using first-order gradient information, avoiding costly second-order Hessian computations. We evaluate our method across various SNN-based architectures, including VGG, ResNet, and Vision Transformer models obtained from both ANN-to-SNN conversion and direct training frameworks, on CIFAR10/100, ImageNet, and DVS-CIFAR-10 datasets. Notably, on ResNet-34 evaluated on ImageNet, our method achieves 73.30% accuracy at 4.78$\times$ compression, which is comparable to that provided by HAWQ-V2, while reducing memory consumption by 2.31$\times$ and bit-allocation runtime by 100$\times$. We also present an energy analysis for mixed-precision SNNs showing that the proposed quantization strategy enables energy-efficient deployment without compromising performance.

</details>


### [35] [CTRL-FM: Inference-Time Structural Control for Flow-Based Virtual Sensing under Long-Horizon Node Outages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30362)
*Boshra Khalili,Andrew Smyth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CTRL-FM：将结构引导视为"由缺失情形决定"的推理期决策的观测保持型流匹配框架，用于长时程节点级传感器中断下的轨迹补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程节点中断使整段传感器轨迹完全缺失，远超常规缺失值插补范畴；且空间/时间依赖的有用性随中断位置与运行工况而变。现有图、扩散、流模型常强加固定结构偏置，易过度平滑突变或传播不可靠邻居误差。

Method: 缺失专用流匹配骨干学习条件速度场，观测值严格固定不变。对每次实际中断，用存活观测构造自监督代理中断，依据其已知真值在无控制、图主导、时间主导、平衡四种控制策略中选择；所选策略由图/时间可靠性（仅由存活观测估计）局部调制，并以边界锚定趋势提供结构引导；控制仅作用于缺失项，若代理证据不足以优于无控制采样则拒绝控制。

Result: 在PeMS08、METR-LA、Air-36、AQI上，30%与60%节点中断下综合表现最优；高达75%传感器丢失的压力测试中退化平缓，稳定优于训练期控制与固定结构控制。

Conclusion: 对每次实际中断进行验证、选择并局部自适应地施加结构引导，比强加单一全局结构先验更有效。

Abstract: Long-horizon node outages are harder than conventional missing-value imputation because complete sensor trajectories remain unobserved for extended periods, while the usefulness of spatial and temporal dependencies varies across outage locations and operating regimes. Existing graph, diffusion, and flow-based methods typically impose a fixed structural bias, which can oversmooth abrupt changes or propagate errors from unreliable neighbors. We introduce CTRL-FM, an observation-preserving flow-matching framework that treats structural guidance as an outage-conditioned inference-time decision. A missing-only flow-matching backbone learns a conditional velocity field while keeping observed measurements exactly fixed. For each realized outage, CTRL-FM constructs self-supervised proxy outages from surviving measurements and uses their known values to select among uncontrolled, graph-dominant, temporal-dominant, and balanced control policies.
