Next, we address the previously unexplored setting of latent variables by developing an instrumental-variable framework for identifying a target direct causal effect under partial observability.

</details>


### [105] [The Value of System 1 Models in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31315)
*Xiufeng Yang,Konstantinos Vrettos,Zeineb Ben Chaaben,Fabian Drexel,Jannik Lübberstedt,Johannes Moll,Anirudh Narayanan,Frederike Stumpf,Cosmin I. Bercea,Jiawen Wang,Evangelia E. Vassalou,Krischan Braitsch,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Markus Graf,Marcus R. Makowski,Lisa Adams,Nassir Navab,Michail E. Klontzas,Keno Bressem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在86个临床任务、20种语言、179,111个案例上评测四个System 1模型（零样本、逐项输出概率），其AUROC在医学知识（0.94）与文本理解（0.93）任务上高，在影像判读（体积病灶0.69）与临床判断上偏低，速度快、能耗低，但跨语言性能骤降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策多为少量固定选项间的快速、高频选择，需不确定性估计以决定何时转交临床医生。生成式语言模型逐字输出、速度慢且置信度校准差；System 1模型可零样本直接输出各选项概率，但其在多临床任务上的整体表现尚不清楚。

Method: 在14个临床领域、20种语言的86个公开任务（含考试题与医学影像）上，对4个System 1模型（2个闭源、2个开源）进行零样本评测，分析前将任务划分为六个能力层级。主要结局为AUROC；次要结局包括校准、语言效应、与已发表结果及生成式模型的比较。

Result: 共179,111例。各任务最优模型的平均AUROC为：医学知识0.94、文本理解0.93、文本临床判断0.83、图像感知0.89、2D影像征象0.84、体积征象0.69；影像任务较文本任务低0.09（95% CI 0.06–0.11，P<0.001）。最优模型在26个文本任务中13个、35个影像任务中8个达到或超过最佳已发表结果。分诊护士准确率0.85优于最优模型0.56。本地开源模型单次决策耗时0.34–0.44秒、能耗24–81焦耳；相同题目从英语到约鲁巴语准确率下降最多39个百分点。同系列生成式模型慢11–76倍，平衡准确率仅高3–5个百分点。

Conclusion: System 1模型决策快速、低成本且置信度可用，适用于答案以文本呈现或需调用医学知识的任务；但在需要主观判断或从影像中读取信息的任务上表现不足。

Abstract: Many clinical decisions are a choice among a few fixed options, taken quickly and in large numbers. Such decisions also need an uncertainty estimate to know when to defer to a clinician. Generative language models answer slowly in free text and state their confidence poorly. In contrast, System 1 models answer zero-shot with a probability for each answer. However, their performance across clinical tasks is unknown. Four System 1 models, two proprietary and two with open weights, were evaluated zero-shot on 86 public tasks from 14 clinical domains in 20 languages, spanning examination questions and medical imaging. Tasks were assigned to six capability tiers before analysis. The primary outcome was the area under the receiver-operating-characteristic curve (AUROC). Secondary outcomes included calibration, language effects and comparisons with published results and generative models. Across 179,111 cases, the mean AUROC of the best model per task was 0.94 for medical knowledge, 0.93 for text understanding, 0.83 for clinical judgement from text, 0.89 for image perception, 0.84 for 2D image findings and 0.69 for volumetric findings. Imaging tasks scored 0.09 (95% CI, 0.06 to 0.11) lower than text tasks (P < 0.001). The best model matched or exceeded the best published result on 13 of 26 text tasks and on 8 of 35 imaging tasks. Triage nurses outperformed the best model (accuracy, 0.85 vs. 0.56). One decision took 0.34 to 0.44 seconds and 24 to 81 joules on open-weight models run locally. Accuracy on identical questions fell by up to 39 percentage points from English to Yoruba. Generative models of the same family were 11 to 76 times slower, with 3 to 5 percentage points higher balanced accuracy. System 1 models decide quickly, cheaply and with usable confidence when the answer is stated in a text or recalled from medical knowledge, but not when it must be judged or read from an image.

</details>


### [106] [Bridging the Data Gap: Source-Informed Task-Space Transport for Few-Shot Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31323)
*Jinjian Jiang,Jing Peng,Rehan Deen,Yu Cao,Ziquan Liu,Muhammad Salman Haleem,David Mguni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Meta-Diff：在紧凑任务空间中学习可复用的“源→目标”关系，用共享编码器将分布映射为低维坐标，学习到的关系把新源坐标输运到预测的目标坐标，再由共享扩散骨干生成，无需针对每个目标重训；并给出该方法成功条件与失败边界的可测分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署（尤其医疗场景，存在持续的人群或采集分布失衡）常需模型从数据丰富的源域快速迁移到仅能稀疏观测的目标域；对每个目标重训或微调大容量生成器代价高，且目标数据稀缺时统计上不可靠。

Method: 1) 共享编码器把每个数据分布映射为低维任务坐标；2) 学习一个跨任务的源→目标关系，将新源坐标输运（transport）到预测的目标坐标；3) 共享扩散骨干在该预测坐标上生成，不做目标特定的重训。并用受控高斯混合实验界定成立条件：任务身份须在表示中可辨、关系须跨任务泛化、源-目标偏移须保留有用的输运余量。

Result: CIFAR-100 留出验证任务上，正确源+输运的平均 SW 比错误源输运低 13.9%；在经验证集选定的设定下做最终单目标对比，平均 SW 比仅目标建模低 14.7%，比全量微调低 32.7%（主路径不使用目标图像且假定适用关系已知）。匹配的 CIFAR 测试回合中，从干净源坐标生成再施加已知腐化优于任务空间输运。Office-Home 暴露互补边界：输运可对齐目标域统计，但对象身份不可靠。

Conclusion: 将极端少样本适应重新表述为任务空间中的可复用输运，并使其成功与失败的条件可直接度量；同时揭示输运与“先干净再退化”两条路径的适用边界。

Abstract: Many deployments require a model to move rapidly from a data-rich source to a related target observed only sparsely. This is especially consequential where target data are difficult to collect, as in healthcare settings with persistent population or acquisition imbalances. Retraining or fine-tuning a high-capacity generator for every target is costly and can be statistically unreliable when target data are scarce. We introduce Meta-Diff, which instead learns a recurring source--target relation once in a compact task space. A shared encoder maps each distribution to a low-dimensional coordinate; a learned relation transports a new source coordinate to its predicted target, and a shared diffusion backbone generates from that prediction without target-specific retraining. Controlled Gaussian-mixture experiments identify when this works: task identity must survive the representation, the relation must generalise across tasks, and the source--target shift must leave useful headroom for transport. On held-out CIFAR-100 validation tasks, correct source $+$ transport achieves 13.9% lower mean SW than transport from the wrong source. With validation-selected adaptation settings in the final-test one-target comparison, its mean SW is 14.7% lower than target-only modelling and 32.7% lower than full fine-tuning; the main route itself uses no target image and assumes the applicable relation is known. On matched CIFAR test episodes, generating from the clean-source coordinate and then applying the known corruption yields lower SW than task-space transport. Office-Home exposes a complementary boundary: transport can align target-domain statistics without reliable object identity. Together, these results recast extreme-few-shot adaptation as reusable transport in task space, while making the conditions for its success and failure directly measurable.

</details>


### [107] [Harmful Responses Are Jailbreak Templates: Transferring Autoregressive Jailbreaks to Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31331)
*Jinwei Gan,Zifeng Cheng,Zhiwei Jiang,Ran Sun,Shiping Ge,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RMT-Jailbreak框架，利用掩码重建机制将自回归模型上的越狱攻击迁移至扩散大语言模型，揭示跨范式攻击迁移风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM的双向上下文建模支持灵活掩码生成，但也因此形成了新的越狱攻击面；作者发现给定查询的有害响应本身即可作为有效的越狱模板。

Method: 将AR生成的有害响应用均匀保留策略转为高掩码率模板，再通过基于提示的安全过滤遮盖残余可见有害内容，最后交由目标dLLM进行双向重建。

Result: 在多种AR越狱方法与多个目标dLLM上实验，验证了RMT-Jailbreak的有效性与通用性，表明越狱攻击具有强的跨范式迁移性。

Conclusion: dLLM的掩码重建特性可被滥用为越狱通道，现有AR攻击可经有害响应迁移至dLLM，需关注跨范式安全对齐问题。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) enable flexible masked generation through bidirectional context modeling, but this capability also introduces a distinct jailbreak attack surface. We find that a harmful response to a given query naturally serves as an effective jailbreak template: simply masking a subset of its tokens can induce a dLLM to reconstruct harmful content. Based on this observation, we propose RMT-Jailbreak, a general framework that transfers existing autoregressive (AR) jailbreak attacks to dLLMs through their induced harmful responses. RMT-Jailbreak converts an AR-generated harmful response into a high-mask-ratio template using uniform token retention, and further applies prompt-based safety filtering to mask any remaining visible harmful content. The refined template is then provided to the target dLLM for bidirectional reconstruction. Extensive experiments across multiple AR jailbreak methods and target dLLMs demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of RMT-Jailbreak, revealing strong cross-paradigm transferability of jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [108] [SQuAT: Spherical Lattice Vector Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31412)
*Sebastian Loeschcke,Ivan Koryakovskiy,Tycho F. A. van der Ouderaa,Riccardo Del Chiaro,Mart van Baalen,Paul N. Whatmough,Markus Nagel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SQuAT：将格向量量化-QAT扩展到十亿参数LLM，通过形状/增益分离的STE实现切空间(隐式Riemannian)更新，并配分布式精确最近邻分配，训练提速12.8×、扩展至10B tokens，取得SOTA VQ-QAT精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格基向量量化(VQ)在低比特LLM上有优秀率失真折衷，但其在量化感知训练(VQ-QAT)下的行为理解不足；且标准STE作用于整体量化器，忽略了形状-增益结构，且VQ-QAT的最近邻赋值难以扩展到大规模训练。

Method: SQuAT将权重分解为方向(shape)与幅度(gain)：归一化方向用E8派生的球面码本量化，增益用独立低比特标量量化器量化；对形状与增益分别施加独立STE，从而对方向产生切空间更新，等价于隐式球面(黎曼)优化。为扩展性设计分布式精确最近邻赋值：各worker量化不相交权重块并仅通信离散索引。

Result: 分布式精确最近邻方案将VQ-QAT训练时间降低12.8×，支持扩展至10B tokens；在指令微调LLaMA-3.2-1B/3B、Qwen3-4B及基座Qwen3-1.7B上，相同量化器与初始化条件下SQuAT一致优于标准VQ-QAT STE，并达到SOTA VQ-QAT精度。

Conclusion: 形状-增益分离的STE是优于整体STE的VQ-QAT参数化方式，可解释为隐式黎曼优化；结合分布式精确赋值，格VQ-QAT可实用地扩展到十亿参数LLM与十亿级token训练。

Abstract: Vector quantization (VQ), in particular lattice-based methods, offer strong rate-distortion trade-offs for low-bit large language models (LLMs), yet their behavior under quantization-aware training (VQ-QAT) remains poorly understood. We scale lattice VQ-QAT to billion-parameter LLMs and multi-billion-token training regimes, and introduce SQuAT, a shape-gain VQ-QAT approach trained with a separate straight-through estimator (STE) for the shape and gain. SQuAT decomposes weights into direction and magnitude: normalized directions are quantized using an $E_8$-derived spherical codebook, while gains are quantized independently with low-bit scalar quantizers. We show that, unlike a standard STE applied to the full quantizer, which ignores the shape-gain structure, these separate STEs induce tangent-space updates for directions, yielding an implicit form of spherical (Riemannian) optimization. To enable scalability, we develop a distributed exact nearest-neighbor assignment scheme, in which workers quantize disjoint weight blocks and communicate only discrete indices, reducing VQ-QAT training time by 12.8$\times$ and allowing us to scale it to 10B tokens. Across instruction-tuned LLaMA-3.2-1B/3B, Qwen3-4B, and base Qwen3-1.7B, SQuAT consistently outperforms standard VQ-QAT STE under identical quantizers and initialization, and achieves SOTA VQ-QAT accuracy.

</details>


### [109] [Post-Hoc Feature Selection Layer for Neural Networks Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31420)
*João Kenji Suwa,João Pedro Silveira e Silva,Bruno Iochins Grisci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者将特征选择层（FSL）改造为训练后即插即用的 post-hoc FSL 模块，在冻结的表格数据预训练模型上学习特征权重，以非侵入方式提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂神经网络的可解释性仍是关键难题，尤其对已部署于高风险场景的模型；作者希望在不动原模型架构与参数的前提下，事后识别原模型最依赖的特征。

Method: 把 FSL 重构为轻量可训练模块，接入已冻结的预训练表格模型，依据原模型输出微调自身权重来学习特征相关性；保持非侵入性（不改变原模型架构和学习参数）。在高维合成与真实表格数据集上，以准确率、F1、召回率、精确率、加权 t-SNE 与轮廓系数等统计与可视化指标评估，并与原始内嵌 FSL 及 Integrated Gradients、Noise Tunnel、DeepLIFT、Gradient SHAP、Feature Ablation 等事后方法对比，同时分析稳定性。

Result: post-hoc FSL 在多个数据集上均成功识别出相关特征，在保持原神经网络预测能力的同时增强了其可解释性；与原始内嵌 FSL 及其他事后可解释性方法的对比结果支持该结论。

Conclusion: post-hoc FSL 是一种非侵入、轻量的事后特征归因方案，可在不牺牲性能的情况下为已部署表格模型提供特征重要性解释，并具备跨数据集的稳定性。

Abstract: The interpretability of complex neural networks remains a critical challenge, especially for models already deployed in high-stakes domains. To address this, we adapted the Feature Selection Layer (FSL) to optimize feature weights post-training, a variant we term post-hoc FSL. Our approach reframes the FSL as a lightweight, trainable module that integrates with already frozen pre-trained models on tabular datasets to highlight the features the original model considers most important. This post-hoc FSL learns feature relevance by fine-tuning its weights based on the original model's learned outputs. Crucially, this process is non-invasive, operating without altering the original model's architecture or its learned parameters. We conducted our experiments using both statistical and visual metrics, including accuracy, F1 score, recall, precision, weighted t-SNE and silhouette score, and also analyzed the stability of the post-hoc FSL on high-dimensional synthetic and real-world tabular datasets. We compared the post-hoc FSL feature weighting method using these metrics against the original embedded FSL and other post-hoc interpretability methods, such as Integrated Gradients, Noise Tunnel, DeepLIFT, Gradient SHAP, and Feature Ablation. Experimental results demonstrate that the post-hoc FSL feature weighting method successfully identified relevant features across the different datasets, maintaining the predictive power of the original neural network while enhancing its interpretability.

</details>


### [110] [Predictable performance is suboptimal, and what it means for model routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31428)
*Guanhua Zhang,Moritz Hardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以模型自身置信度（对所选答案的概率）为基线，分析性能预测器在模型路由中究竟能带来多大增益，并从理论上证明：预测器相对置信度的优势可等价转化为经重校准后的模型log loss改进，且该优势受"模型次优性 + 额外参数带来的增益"所界定；在16个模型、4个基准上的实验表明，预测器的优势在越次优的模型上越大，并随微调提升而收缩，级联路由呈现相同规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在能力与成本上存在差异，需要为每个查询自适应地选择模型（路由）。现有路由方法普遍依赖"性能预测"——预测某模型是否会答对——并单独训练候选模型的性能预测器。但作者质疑：性能预测器究竟在多大程度上优于一个极其廉价的基线（模型自身置信度），这一增益的来源与上界尚未被清晰刻画。

Method: 1) 以模型对所选答案的置信度概率作为基线预测器；2) 理论上证明任何优于置信度的性能预测器，其优势可通过重校准（recalibration）精确转移到模型的log loss改进上（log loss意义下优势完全传递）；3) 在计入预测器额外参数容量的前提下，给出其优势的上界=模型的次优性（suboptimality）+额外参数带来的增益；4) 将理论结果推广到两模型级联（cascade）：当预测第一模型将失败时将查询路由至更强模型；5) 在16个语言模型、4个基准上实证检验，并考察微调前后的变化。

Result: 实验显示：预测器相对置信度的优势在模型越次优时越大；随模型经微调改进，该优势缩小。级联路由呈现同样两种模式：基于预测器的路由相对基于置信度的路由在更次优模型上收益更大，且微调后收益衰减。

Conclusion: 性能预测只有在候选模型对任务次优、或预测器相对候选模型引入显著额外容量时才优于简单基线；模型自身置信度是路由中强而廉价的基线。该结论对单模型选择与两模型级联均成立，提示若模型本身已较优，复杂的性能预测器收益有限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) differ in capabilities and costs, motivating routing methods that adaptively choose a model for each query. These methods rely on performance prediction: given a query, estimate whether a model will answer it correctly. Most routing methods train separate predictors of candidate models' performance to guide model selection. Our work shows that performance prediction only beats a simple baseline when the candidate model is suboptimal on the task or the performance predictor adds significant capacity relative to the candidate model. Specifically, we take a model's self-confidence, the probability it assigns to its chosen answer, as a baseline and study how much a performance predictor can improve on it. First, we show that any performance predictor that outperforms a model's self-confidence can be used to improve the model's loss: the predictor's advantage transfers exactly in log loss through recalibration. Second, accounting for the predictor's additional parameters, we bound its advantage by the model's suboptimality plus the gain from those parameters. Third, we extend our theoretical results to two-model cascades, which route queries to a more capable model when the first model is predicted to fail. Experiments with 16 language models on four benchmarks show that predictors gain a larger advantage over self-confidence on more suboptimal models. This advantage shrinks as models improve through finetuning. Cascading exhibits the same two patterns: predictor-based routing offers larger gains over confidence-based routing for more suboptimal models, and these gains diminish after finetuning.

</details>


### [111] [Drift Says When, Budget Says How Much: Rate-Controlled KV Re-selection for Long-Form Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31433)
*Bin Hu,Yingbo Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多模态大模型KV cache压缩中「预填充阶段一次性选择视觉token」在长文本生成中严重失效；作者提出速率受控刷新（RCR），用漂移触发重选、由每样本控制器调控刷新频率，在所有模型任务上以单套超参超越InfiniGen 1.6–3.8 ROUGE-L点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向多模态LLM的KV cache压缩方法在prefill阶段一次性选定需要保留的视觉token，并在整个生成过程中复用。作者发现该一次性选择在长文本生成（如详细图像描述）中失效：即使使用未压缩答案的注意力做oracle选择，与未压缩输出的ROUGE-L一致性也仅0.44–0.52，原因是模型所依赖的证据会随描述推进而转移。直观补救是「注意力漂移出保留集即重选」，但固定漂移阈值会系统性失效：漂移幅度在样本间不可比，低漂移样本几乎从不刷新，而这类样本恰是受益最大者。

Method: 提出速率受控刷新（Rate-Controlled Refresh, RCR）：由漂移决定「何时、何处」重选，同时用每样本控制器调节「多久刷新一次」的刷新频率，从而规避固定阈值的跨样本不可比问题；并辅以低秩漂移检测器、候选受限的重选（candidate-restricted re-selection）以及保持在GPU上完成的决策流程（单一设置适用于所有模型与任务）。

Result: 在六个模型-任务设置中的五个上，RCR比逐步骤检索方法InfiniGen高1.6–3.8 ROUGE-L点，第六个无显著差异；与精确逐步骤重选差距在1.7点以内；每步估计数据搬运量相当，GPU常驻检测器小4倍。DetailCaps上，相同刷新率下按漂移选时机优于随机时机2.0–5.0点，也优于更高刷新率的周期式时机。预注册的视频描述任务中漂移跨样本同质，RCR在Qwen2-VL上与InfiniGen持平、在Qwen3-VL上超越之，但在最近测试刷新率下不再胜过固定阈值，与诊断一致；对象级评测显示RCR在三个模型中的两个上相对未压缩输出取得更高object F1。

Conclusion: 一次性视觉token选择不适合长文本生成，证据随时间漂移；固定漂移阈值因跨样本漂移幅度不可比而失效，需引入速率控制。RCR以单套超参在多设置上稳定优于InfiniGen并逼近精确逐步骤重选，且开销相当、检测器更小；漂移触发时机的优势在漂移异质场景明显，在漂移同质场景（如视频描述）则与固定阈值相当，印证了方法诊断的适用边界。

Abstract: KV cache compression for multimodal LLMs usually selects the visual tokens to keep once, at prefill, and reuses that selection for the whole generation. We show that this one-shot selection is severely limited on long-form generation: on detailed image description, even an oracle that selects with the attention of the uncompressed answer reaches only 0.44-0.52 ROUGE-L agreement with the uncompressed output, because the evidence the model reads moves as the description unfolds. Re-selecting whenever attention drifts out of the kept set is the natural remedy, but we find that a fixed drift threshold fails systematically: drift magnitudes are not comparable across samples, so low-drift samples are almost never refreshed although they benefit the most. We propose Rate-Controlled Refresh (RCR), which lets drift decide when and where to re-select while a per-sample controller regulates how often, combined with a low-rank drift detector, candidate-restricted re-selection, and a decision that stays on the GPU. With one setting for all models and tasks, RCR outperforms the per-step retrieval method InfiniGen by 1.6-3.8 ROUGE-L points on five of six model-task settings with no significant difference on the sixth, while staying within 1.7 points of exact per-step re-selection, with comparable estimated data movement per step and a 4x smaller GPU-resident detector. On DetailCaps, drift-chosen timing beats random timing at the same refresh rate and periodic timing even at a higher rate, by 2.0-5.0 points. On pre-registered video description, where drift is homogeneous across samples, RCR is on par with InfiniGen on Qwen2-VL and exceeds it on Qwen3-VL but no longer beats a fixed threshold at the nearest tested rates, consistent with the diagnosis; object-level evaluation shows that RCR achieves higher object F1 against the uncompressed output than InfiniGen on two of three models.

</details>


### [112] [Multi-Token Prediction and Alternative Tokenization Improve Learning Latent Multi-Hop Graph Search at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31439)
*Arvid Frydenlund,FuTe Wong,Katherine Lambert,Frank Rudzicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出语言模型学习最短路任务的失败源于监督信号与分词两个正交原因，并用多token预测（MTP）与替代分词突破此前的规模瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定未见图、起点s与终点t，语言模型能否生成最短路径？先前工作发现随图规模增大模型无法学会该任务，本文旨在解释并解决这一失败。

Method: 理论上建立并实证验证多token预测（MTP）的有效性，表明其收益与拓扑无关；同时引入替代分词方案，并证明该两因素相互正交。

Result: 结合MTP与替代分词，突破了先前工作观察到的规模墙，在远超此前极限的规模上持续、稳定地学会求解该任务。

Conclusion: 语言模型在该任务上的规模失效并非不可克服，监督信号（MTP）与分词是两个可分离的致因，二者结合可显著扩展可学习的图规模。

Abstract: Given an unseen graph, a start node, $s$, and an end node, $t$, can a language model generate the shortest path from $s$ to $t$? Prior work has found that models fail to learn this task as the graphs scale. We show that this failure has two causes: the supervision signal and the tokenization. First, we theoretically establish and empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of multi-token prediction (MTP), and show that the benefits of MTP are topologically agnostic. Second, we introduce alternative tokenizations and show that the two causes are orthogonal. Using these methods, we break through the scaling wall observed by prior work, consistently and stably learning to solve the task at scales far beyond previous limits.

</details>


### [113] [RheOPT: Few-Shot Non-Newtonian Flow Prediction via Newtonian Pretraining of RheOFormer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31465)
*Maedeh Saberi,Amir Barati Farimani,Safa Jamali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RheOPT：先用廉价牛顿流体仿真预训练Transformer神经算子，再用少量非牛顿样本微调，实现数据高效的非牛顿流场算子学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非牛顿流动仿真因速度与应力需通过率相关、历史相关的本构动力学耦合推进而计算昂贵；神经算子替代模型虽降低推理成本，但其训练数据仍依赖同样昂贵的仿真，因此在仅能承担少量非牛顿仿真时，数据高效学习至关重要。

Method: 构建基于Transformer的神经算子RheOPT，在廉价牛顿仿真上预训练，再在少量非牛顿样本上微调；不迁移牛顿本构响应本身，而是复用应力模型改变后仍有用的几何条件化输运结构。在两种几何匹配设定中评估：4:1收缩流道（Giesekus、Oldroyd-B、Bingham流体）与三角障碍后振荡尾流（Oldroyd-B）；并比较RevIN与Global归一化的影响，辅以相位不敏感诊断。

Result: 收缩流道中牛顿预训练显著改善少样本预测：仅用4个目标样本微调的模型可匹配甚至超过用4倍样本从零训练的模型；该收益在RevIN下稳定，在Global归一化下依赖数据集，差异源于各流体速度/应力场的动态范围。尾流中，相对L2误差不足以验证涡脱落的恢复，相位不敏感诊断显示预训练恢复了少样本下从零模型所压制的脱落频率与尾流脉动幅值。

Conclusion: RheOPT确立了牛顿仿真作为具有物理依据的预训练来源，可用于数据高效的非牛顿算子学习；同时指出归一化策略与评估指标（需相位不敏感诊断）对结论可靠性的关键影响。

Abstract: Despite advances in numerical methods, non-Newtonian flow simulations remain computationally expensive because velocity and stress must be advanced together through rate- and history-dependent constitutive dynamics. Neural-operator surrogates reduce inference cost after training, but their training data require the same expensive simulations they aim to replace, making data-efficient learning essential when only a few non-Newtonian simulations can be afforded. Here, we introduce the Rheological Operator Pretrained Transformer (RheOPT), which pretrains a transformer-based neural operator on inexpensive Newtonian simulations and fine-tunes it on a small number of non-Newtonian samples. Rather than transferring the Newtonian constitutive response itself, RheOPT reuses geometry-conditioned transport structure that remains useful after the stress model changes. We evaluate RheOPT in two geometry-matched settings: a $4{:}1$ contraction channel for Giesekus, Oldroyd-B, and Bingham fluids, and the oscillatory wake behind a triangular obstacle for Oldroyd-B flow. In the contraction, Newtonian pretraining substantially improves few-shot prediction, and a model fine-tuned on four target samples can match or exceed a from-scratch model trained on four times as many. This benefit is reliable under reversible instance normalization (RevIN) but dataset dependent under Global normalization, a difference we trace to the dynamic range of each fluid's velocity and stress fields. In the wake, where relative $L_2$ error alone cannot verify that vortex shedding is recovered, phase-insensitive diagnostics show that pretraining recovers the shedding frequency and wake fluctuation amplitude that are suppressed by the from-scratch model in the few-shot regime. Overall, RheOPT establishes Newtonian simulation as a physically grounded pretraining source for data-efficient non-Newtonian operator learning.

</details>


### [114] [Evaluating the Applicability of LLM Inference Optimizations on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31479)
*Kyrylo Yemets,Andrii Yaroshevych,Victor Muryn,Ostap Khomenko,Pavlo Kryven,Maksym Kmet,Maksym Shamrai,Mariya Hirna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估四类主流LLM推理优化（KV-cache剪枝、投机解码、移植GPU kernel、训练后量化）在Apple Silicon本地单请求解码场景下的实际收益，发现多数在服务器GPU上验证的加速并不可迁移，加速比是"技术×运行时×硬件契约×工作负载"的联合函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon是最广泛的本地LLM推理平台之一，但几乎所有推理优化都在服务器级NVIDIA GPU的批量服务负载下设计与验证。因此需要回答：哪些优化迁移到Apple Silicon后仍保留收益，以及收益由什么决定。

Method: 在同构的单请求解码负载下，于四台Apple设备上评测四类优化族（KV-cache剪枝、投机解码、移植GPU kernel、训练后量化），并在可行处与RTX 4090做匹配对照。

Result: KV-cache剪枝在Apple上端到端吞吐最高提升1.75倍，但在桌面级NVIDIA GPU上反而可能降低吞吐；所有评测的投机解码方法在Apple上均降低吞吐，尽管在服务器GPU上有2–6倍加速报告；CUDA kernel设计仅部分可迁移（公开Metal缺少异步拷贝，batch=1解码使多种面向服务的机制失效），可迁移组件最多带来1.20倍解码吞吐；量化收益因机器而异。

Conclusion: 报告中的加速比不是技术本身的便携属性，而是技术、运行时、硬件契约与工作负载区间的联合函数；优化迁移性需按此四要素联合判断。

Abstract: Apple Silicon is among the most widely used platforms for local LLM inference, yet nearly all inference optimizations are designed and validated on server-class NVIDIA GPUs under batched serving workloads. Which optimizations retain their benefits when moved to Apple Silicon, and what determines their applicability? We evaluate four optimization families: KV-cache pruning, speculative decoding, ported GPU kernels, and post-training quantization. Experiments span four Apple machines under a common single-request decode workload, with matched RTX 4090 comparisons where possible. KV-cache pruning can reach up to 1.75 times end-to-end throughput on Apple but can reduce throughput on desktop NVIDIA GPUs. Under the common benchmark, all evaluated speculative decoding methods reduce throughput on Apple, despite reported 2-6 times gains on server GPUs. CUDA kernel designs transfer only partially: public Metal lacks asynchronous copy, and batch-one decode makes several serving-oriented mechanisms inapplicable, although transferable components yield up to 1.20 times decode throughput. Quantization gains vary by machine. Across four families, a reported speedup is not a portable property of a technique alone but a joint function of technique, runtime, hardware contract, and workload regime.

</details>


### [115] [Training Small Language Models for Transferable Tool Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31483)
*Zhaoyi Zhou,Akshayaa Magesh,Jeongyeol Kwon,Artem Kuriksha,Daniel Jiang,Andrea Zanette,Ayush Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 小规模开源模型Qwen3-8B经强化学习后训练即可实现"驱动检索器+分解指令+选择工具"的功能，在ToolBench上超过Llama-4-Maverick(402B)，并可跨检索器迁移、跨领域工具语料泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent面对上万工具时，工具描述无法全部放入上下文，且基于相关性的检索器只能排序、不知哪些工具真正被需要；现有方案依赖Claude、ChatGPT等商业闭源大模型来驱动检索器，成本与可及性受限。

Method: 用强化学习（RL）对小型开源模型Qwen3-8B进行后训练，使其复现商业LLM的检索驱动能力：分解复杂多步指令，并在检索出的候选工具上进行选择。

Result: 在ToolBench上，经该配方后训练的Qwen3-8B优于Llama-4-Maverick（402B）；训练后的模型可直接迁移到不同检索器，并泛化到不同领域的新工具语料。

Conclusion: 无需依赖专有商业大模型，小规模开源模型经RL后训练即可胜任大规模工具选择中的指令分解与候选筛选，且具备跨检索器与跨领域的可迁移性。

Abstract: As LLM agents operate over corpora of more than ten thousand tools, selecting the right tool for a complex multi-step user instruction becomes a central bottleneck. Tool descriptions cannot all fit in context, and relevance-based retrievers only rank tools without knowing which are actually needed. Recent work instead leverages proprietary commercial LLMs like Claude or ChatGPT to drive a retriever, decomposing the instruction and selecting over retrieved candidates. We show that a small, open model can be trained via reinforcement learning to perform the same functionality. On ToolBench, a Qwen3-8B model post-trained with our recipe outperforms Llama-4-Maverick (402B). In addition, the trained model transfers directly to different retrievers and generalizes to new tool corpora in different domains.

</details>


### [116] [SABLE: Scalable Affine Bayesian Latent-space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31495)
*Erik Bodin,Lisa Maria Kreusser,Donney Fan,Henry Moss,Geoff Pleiss,Nicolas Durrande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SABLE 提出一种面向高维潜空间的贝叶斯优化方法，用贝叶斯仿射代理模型将查询分解为跨度内EI优化与跨度外标量范数调度，兼顾样本效率与毫秒级决策时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计、蛋白质等复杂对象的设计空间高维且目标函数评估昂贵；生成模型的潜空间维度D常远大于评估预算，导致传统高维贝叶斯优化难以适用。

Method: 采用贝叶斯仿射代理模型进行预测与采集，将高阶结构视为代理偏差；N次观测后斜率更新的维度至多N-1，从而把每个查询分解为至多N维的跨度内分量（用期望改进EI优化）与由标量范数控制的跨度外分量。指出仿射EI隐式选择的跨度外范数会导致探索不足，改用有限时域调度显式指定该范数，且不将搜索限制在学习子空间；并用重压缩方案将信息归约为O(M)个伪观测，使重拟合代价上界为O(M²D+M³)。

Result: 在合成基准与潜空间优化任务上取得高样本效率，并在大预算下将决策时间降低数个数量级，参数推断、预测与采集决策保持在数十毫秒量级。

Conclusion: SABLE 为高维各向同性潜空间中有限预算的贝叶斯优化提供了高效方案，通过仿射代理分解与范数调度解决了探索不足问题，同时借助重压缩实现可扩展的计算成本。

Abstract: Complex objects such as engineering designs and proteins are often difficult to optimise because their design spaces exhibit high-dimensional structure and objective evaluations are expensive. Generative models provide continuous latent representations, but their latent spaces can have thousands to tens of thousands of dimensions, creating a regime where the latent dimension $D$ greatly exceeds the evaluation budget. We introduce SABLE, a Bayesian optimisation method for high-dimensional latent spaces with an isotropic distribution and limited budget. SABLE uses a Bayesian affine surrogate for prediction and acquisition, treating higher-order structure as surrogate discrepancy. After $N$ observations, the slope update has dimension at most $N-1$, yielding a decomposition of each query into an in-span component optimised by expected improvement (EI) in at most $N$ dimensions and an off-span component governed by a scalar norm. We show that the off-span norm implicitly selected by affine EI can lead to underexploration, and SABLE instead prescribes it using a finite-horizon schedule, without restricting search to a learned subspace. A recompression scheme reduces information to $\mathcal O(M)$ pseudo-observations, bounding refitting cost by $\mathcal O(M^2D+M^3)$ while keeping parameter inference, prediction, and acquisition decisions to tens of milliseconds. Across synthetic benchmarks and latent-space optimisation tasks, SABLE achieves high sample efficiency and reduces decision time by several orders of magnitude at large budgets.

</details>


### [117] [Piano2Pop: Expanding Piano Demos into Pop Productions Without Aligned Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31510)
*Yun-Chen Cheng,Chih-Pin Tan,Tzu-Hung Huang,Yi-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Piano2Pop音乐扩展任务将钢琴MIDI转换为器乐流行编曲，用伪监督方式在无对齐钢琴-流行配对下微调预训练flow-matching模型，在476对真实数据上验证优于零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期音乐生成与复音转谱的进展使"钢琴→完整流行编曲"的自动音乐扩展成为可能，但该任务缺乏对齐的钢琴-流行配对监督数据，因此需要探索伪监督与成对监督两条可行路线。

Method: 从流行录音中合成钢琴-器乐配对数据训练；采用伪监督策略，将预训练flow-matching模型以钢琴卷帘为条件，无需对齐的钢琴-流行监督。对比两种伪演示源：无配对转谱（含函数选择增强FST与音区密度增强RDA）与成对监督的钢琴翻奏生成器。

Result: 在476对真实钢琴翻奏/流行曲对上测试，成对监督方案在真实钢琴输入上取得最优FAD、伴奏提示遵循度与频谱形状；FST在无配对训练中缓解输出过于"钢琴化"，是颇具前景的替代方案；所有微调系统均显著优于零样本基线。

Conclusion: 任务特定建模对Piano2Pop有效且必要；伪监督可绕开对齐数据瓶颈，成对监督效果最佳而FST为无配对场景提供可行折中。

Abstract: We introduce Piano2Pop, a music-expansion task that transforms piano MIDI into instrumental pop productions. Enabled by recent advances in music generation and polyphonic music transcription, our pseudo-supervised approach conditions a pretrained flow-matching model on piano rolls without aligned piano-pop supervision. We train on synthetic piano-instrumental pairs derived from pop recordings, comparing pair-free transcription, including function-selective (FST) and register-density (RDA) augmentations, with a pair-supervised piano-cover generator as pseudo-demo sources. On 476 testing real-world piano-cover/pop pairs, the pair-supervised approach achieves the best Frechet Audio Distance, Accompaniment Prompt Adherence, and spectral shape on real-piano inputs, while FST mitigates piano-heavy outputs in pair-free training and remains a promising alternative. All fine-tuned systems significantly outperform the zero-shot baselines, which suggests a role for task-specific modeling.

</details>


### [118] [PYTHIA, a transferable machine learning framework for simultaneous defect and adsorption site optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31539)
*Ioannis Kouroudis,Zhaosu Gu,Poonam,Leon Muller,Alessio Gagliardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PYTHIA框架，结合基础模型、迁移学习、子空间对齐（Transformer层）与高斯过程不确定性量化，在少量样本下用于新材料的贝叶斯优化，并在两个体系的吸附与缺陷优化中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在材料优化中价值显著，但其对大量样本的依赖限制了其在研究较少、新出现的材料家族中的应用。

Method: 采用在化学相似分子大规模数据集上训练、且预测目标物理上接近的基础模型；冻结其权重，通过Transformer层进行子空间对齐，并用高斯过程加入不确定性量化；这两个附加层可在有限数据上训练，从而复用已有知识与复杂架构；所得模型作为贝叶斯优化代理模型。

Result: 在两个不同体系的吸附与缺陷优化任务中验证框架的鲁棒性；能够正确且高效地识别即使是复杂体系的吸附位点（摘要此处被截断，其余性能细节未给出）。

Conclusion: PYTHIA通过基础模型加轻量可训练层的迁移策略，可在小样本条件下将机器学习拓展至新颖且研究不足的材料体系，并支撑贝叶斯优化流程。

Abstract: Machine learning has permeated material optimization, providing high value. Nevertheless, its re- liance on a high number of samples limits its applicability to relatively explored material families. In this paper we extend the applicability of machine learning to novel and understudied materials through the PYTHIA (Probabilistic Yet Transferable Hierarchical Interaction Architecture) framework. Pythia utilizes two of the most powerful machine learning concepts, foundational models and transfer learning. We employ a complex foundational model trained on a large dataset of chemically similar molecules, on a target that is physically close to our prediction target. Then, we freeze the weights and perform subspace alignment with the use of a transformer layer and add uncertainty quantification with gaussian processes. These two layers can be trained on a limited dataset yet allow us to leverage the gained knowl- edge and the studied materials and complex architectures. The resulting model is used as a surrogate for a Bayesian Optimization scheme. To showcase the robustness of the framework, we applied it to the investigation of adsorption and defect optimization of two distinct systems. Not only were we able to correctly and efficiently identify the optimal adsorption sites of even complex a

</details>


### [119] [Same Luck, Different Choice: Twin-World Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31544)
*Zeropy Surio,Tobias Engel,Mei-Lin Chou,Anika Raman,Samuel Okafor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWICE通过"孪生世界"噪声耦合实现无偏、低方差的回合内信用估计，在七个问答基准上显著超越GRPO与树搜索/反事实分支基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习只对整条轨迹给奖励，而策略需要为回合内每一步决策分配信用。反事实分支虽可估计信用，但分支采样噪声与事实轨迹独立，导致结果差异混入了决策效应与后续随机性（"运气"）的影响，估计噪声大。

Method: 在智能体检索场景中检索器、语料与评分器均确定性，回合是策略决策与采样噪声的确定函数，因此可实现"孪生世界"。TWICE按轮次与行对每个token的Gumbel噪声加键，展开继承事实轨迹下游噪声的反事实孪生轨迹，将配对结果差转为轮级优势，用于actor采样轨迹的策略梯度更新（仅部署actor）；并采用平方根规则按单位成本预测分歧处分配孪生预算。

Result: 孪生信用对任意耦合质量无偏；方差为独立反事实方差乘以1-ρ（ρ为孪生结果相关性）；耦合下解析规模为Δ的优势所需展开数从Θ(Δ^-2)降至Θ(Δ^-1)。在七个问答基准上，Qwen2.5-3B/7B上平均精确匹配较GRPO分别提升5.3与4.5分，优于树搜索与反事实分支基线，辅助token较VinePPO少68%。

Conclusion: 通过噪声键控的孪生反事实展开，可在确定性智能体搜索环境中获得无偏且低方差的轮级信用，显著提升样本效率与最终性能，同时降低辅助计算开销。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning rewards a complete episode, yet the policy needs credit for every decision inside it. Counterfactual branching estimates this credit by re-running the episode after an alternative decision, but the branch draws its sampling noise independently of the factual episode, so the outcome difference mixes the effect of the decision with the luck of everything that follows. We observe that in agentic search the retriever, the corpus, and the grader are deterministic, so an episode is a deterministic function of the policy's decisions and its sampling noise. Twin worlds are therefore realizable: the same noise, a different decision. We introduce TWICE (TWIn-world Credit Estimation), which keys the Gumbel noise of every token by turn and line, rolls out counterfactual twins that inherit the factual episode's downstream noise, and turns their paired outcome differences into turn-level advantages for policy-gradient updates on actor-sampled trajectories; only the actor is deployed. Twin credit is unbiased for any coupling quality, its variance is that of an independent counterfactual scaled by $1-ρ$, where $ρ$ is the twins' outcome correlation, and under coupling the number of rollouts needed to resolve an advantage of size $Δ$ falls from $Θ(Δ^{-2})$ to $Θ(Δ^{-1})$. A square-root rule spends the twin budget where outcomes are predicted to disagree per unit cost. On seven question-answering benchmarks, TWICE improves the average exact match of GRPO by 5.3 and 4.5 points on Qwen2.5-3B and 7B, outperforms tree-search and counterfactual-branching baselines, and uses 68% fewer auxiliary tokens than VinePPO.

</details>


### [120] [Shift-aware score transport for privacy auditing without retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31549)
*Yaohong Yang,Gauri Pradhan,Qi Chen,Samuel Kaski,Antti Honkela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在观察式审计设定下，针对成员推断分数因非成员分布偏移而失真的问题，提出基于表征空间运输并辅以保守校正的审计方法，获得更稳定且更紧的隐私下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察式审计场景（无法插入canary、无法控制数据纳入、无法重训练）中，可用非成员与训练数据分布不一致，导致成员推断攻击分数产生偏移，从而使隐私损失下界估计失真。

Method: 从OOD非成员中学习"数据表征—成员推断分数"的关系，并将其运输到目标协变量分布以估计反事实的同分布非成员分数分布；针对外推与条件不变性不完美导致的运输不可靠，引入直接对齐名义运输隐私水平的保守校正，构造受控校准任务估计表征空间位移对审计的膨胀效应并据此修正运输误差。

Result: 在多个存在分布偏移的数据集上，方法给出显著更稳定的隐私估计，同时相比基线保持更紧的下界。

Conclusion: 通过在表征空间进行分布运输并施加校准的保守校正，可在无法干预训练的观察式审计中有效缓解非成员分布偏移带来的偏差，实现可靠且紧致的隐私审计。

Abstract: Privacy auditing empirically assesses privacy leakage by using membership inference attacks to establish lower bounds on privacy loss. We consider an observational auditing setting in which the auditor cannot insert canaries, control data inclusion, or retrain the target model, and must instead rely on fixed sets of known members and non-members. This setting is relevant for large deployed models or when retraining is restricted by computational or access constraints. In this case, non-members available for auditing may differ in distribution from the training data, causing membership-inference scores to be skewed due to the distribution shift. We address this problem by estimating the counterfactual in-distribution non-member score distribution using the available out-of-distribution (OOD) non-members. Our proposed method learns the relationship between data representations and membership-inference scores from OOD non-members and transports it to the target covariate distribution. Because this transport may become unreliable under extrapolation or imperfect conditional invariance, we further introduce a conservative correction calibrated directly to the nominal transported privacy level. We construct controlled calibration tasks from the available non-member data to estimate how representation-space displacement inflates the audit, and use this estimate to correct the transport-induced audit error. Across multiple datasets with distribution shifts, our method yields substantially more stable privacy estimates while retaining tighter lower bounds than baselines.

</details>


### [121] [Fine-Grained Analysis of Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Learning via Algorithmic Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31551)
*Guodong Zheng,Yan Sun,Peng Wang,Enneng Yang,Jiafei Lyu,Xiaochun Cao,Dacheng Tao,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文构建了SGD下的算法稳定性框架，将期望遗忘分解为任务内/跨任务泛化差与经验遗忘，给出不依赖特定模型结构的遗忘上界，并通过CIFAR-100/MiniImageNet实验验证理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中的灾难性遗忘是关键难题，但现有理论分析多依赖特定模型或任务结构，且难以刻画迭代优化过程如何影响遗忘。

Method: 提出不预设模型架构、基于SGD的算法稳定性框架：将遗忘分解为任务内泛化差、跨任务泛化差与经验遗忘；通过追踪样本扰动在任务内更新及后续任务间的传播来界定泛化差，比较常数、任务内衰减、持续衰减三种学习率调度以刻画任务边界重启衰减的影响；并以沿优化轨迹的累积梯度对齐界定经验遗忘，无需凸性假设。

Result: 得到期望遗忘的上界，显式包含训练集规模、每任务更新次数、任务总数、学习率调度与累积梯度对齐的影响，并在Split CIFAR-100与Split MiniImageNet上验证其经验含义。

Conclusion: 算法稳定性为理解迭代优化与遗忘的关系提供了通用理论工具，学习率调度与梯度对齐是控制遗忘的关键因素。

Abstract: Understanding catastrophic forgetting, in which learning new tasks degrades performance on previously learned tasks, remains a fundamental challenge in continual learning (CL). Existing theoretical analyses often rely on specific model or task structures and offer limited insight into how iterative optimization affects forgetting. To address this gap, we develop an algorithmic stability framework for expected population forgetting under SGD without prescribing a particular model architecture. Specifically, we decompose forgetting into intra-task and cross-task generalization gaps and empirical forgetting. To bound the generalization gaps, we track how a sample perturbation affects updates within a task and propagates through subsequent tasks. We consider constant, intra-task decaying, and continuously decaying learning-rate schedules. Comparing the two decaying schedules allows us to characterize how restarting learning-rate decay at task boundaries affects the propagation of historical sample perturbations. For empirical forgetting, we derive bounds for all three schedules by retaining cumulative gradient alignment along the optimization trajectory, without requiring convexity. Combining these results yields upper bounds on expected forgetting that capture the effects of training set size, the number of updates per task, the total number of tasks, learning-rate schedules, and cumulative gradient alignment. Finally, we conduct experiments on Split CIFAR-100 and Split MiniImageNet to examine the empirical implications of our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [122] [Diffusion Language Models for Perplexity-Aware Black-Box Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31558)
*Ali Al-Lawati,Elsayed Eshra,Dongwon Lee,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将连续潜空间攻击框架直接耦合到扩散语言模型(dLLM)，用学习到的语言生成分布替换人工随机映射，在保持标准高斯潜空间与采样/优化流程的同时，使生成的攻击提示更自然（困惑度降至3–5），并发现不同映射会导致风险估计相差一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续空间攻击在低维高斯潜空间中采样或优化，再通过"随机映射+最近邻token查表"解码为文本。由于随机映射并非从语言中学习，产生的提示高度不自然，易被输入护栏检测，且其风险估计不基于自然提示，缺乏真实性。

Method: 把同一连续潜空间框架直接耦合到学习得到的语言生成分布——连续扩散语言模型(dLLM)——以替代人工随机映射，同时保留标准高斯潜空间表述及下游采样/优化流程。在两个设定下评估：基于采样的RareTrap与基于优化的ThinkTrap搜索。

Result: RareTrap中字符级困惑度从10^3–10^7降至3–5；ThinkTrap中从20–28降至4.3–5.4。两种映射下的模型风险估计可相差一个数量级。

Conclusion: 用dLLM替换随机映射可显著提升攻击提示的自然性，但映射选择会实质性影响风险估计，说明基于不自然提示的风险评估不可靠，潜空间攻击评估需以自然语言分布为基准。

Abstract: \emph{Continuous-space} attacks find prompts that trigger malicious behaviors, such as overthinking, in black-box LLMs by sampling or optimizing over a low-dimensional Gaussian latent space and decoding it to text through a \emph{random mapping} and nearest-token lookup. Because the random mapping is not learned from language, its prompts can be highly unnatural, and as such easily detectable through input guardrails, and the risk estimates they yield are not grounded in natural prompts. In this paper, we demonstrate that the same continuous latent-space framework can be coupled directly to a learned generative distribution over language, a continuous diffusion language model (dLLM), which removes the artificial random mapping while preserving the standard-Gaussian latent formulation and the downstream sampling or optimization procedure. We evaluate this approach under two settings: sampling-based estimation using \emph{RareTrap} and optimization-based search using \emph{ThinkTrap}. We find that character-level perplexity drops from $10^3$--$10^7$ to 3--5 in RareTrap and from 20--28 to 4.3--5.4 in ThinkTrap. We also find that model risk estimates can differ by an order of magnitude across the two mappings.

</details>


### [123] [When to Follow the Teacher: Advantage-Based On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31563)
*Mahdi Sabbaghi,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani,Adel Javanmard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出优势感知的在线策略蒸馏（AOPD），用学生视角下的反事实优势对教师动作加权，仅依赖学生标准 rollout 与终端奖励即可无偏估计，无需额外 rollout，在数学、代码与 MedQA 上优于蒸馏和 RL 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在轨迹每个状态都要求学生跟随教师，隐含假设教师指导总是有益；但教师偏好的动作未必真正有助于当前学生取得成功，因此需要区分"教师偏好"与"对学生实际有帮助"的监督信号。

Method: 提出 AOPD：以教师动作在当前学生下的反事实优势为权重，强化能提升学生期望结果的动作、降权其余动作。为避免逐步尝试替代教师动作并重新 rollout 的高昂代价，作者证明该目标可仅用学生标准 rollout 及其终端奖励估计：在学生采样动作上使用教师重要性权重，构造无偏的优势加权监督，不增加 rollout 或奖励评估。

Result: 在数学、代码（coding）和 MedQA 任务上，AOPD 相较蒸馏与强化学习基线均取得提升，且在外置教师与自蒸馏两种设置下均有效。

Conclusion: AOPD 将教师监督聚焦于真正有利于当前学生的动作，同时保持标准 OPD 的 rollout 效率，是一种兼顾指导质量与训练成本的在线蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Online Policy Distillation (OPD) often outperforms offline methods such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) by training on trajectories sampled directly from the student. However, standard OPD trains the student to follow the teacher at every state of the trajectory, assuming that teacher guidance is always beneficial. We instead distinguish between what the teacher prefers and what actually helps the current student succeed. We introduce Advantage-Based On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which weights teacher actions by their counterfactual advantage under the student. Teacher actions that improve the student's expected outcome are reinforced, while those that do not are downweighted.
  Directly computing these advantages requires trying alternative teacher actions at each step and running new rollouts from each. Instead, we show that the AOPD objective can be estimated using only standard student rollouts and their terminal rewards. Our estimator uses teacher importance weights on the student-sampled actions to provide unbiased advantage-weighted supervision, without any additional rollouts or reward evaluations. This allows AOPD to focus teacher supervision on actions that help the current student while preserving the rollout efficiency of standard OPD. Experiments on math, coding, and Medqa show gains over distillation and RL baselines in both external-teacher and self-distillation settings.

</details>


### [124] [On the Token Collapse Phenomenon in Textual Graph Soft Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31572)
*Andrea Giuseppe Di Francesco,Rishabh Jain,Franco Maria Nardini,Nicola Tonellotto,Pietro Liò,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文深入分析图软提示方法 G-Retriever，发现其图 token 在不同输入间几乎不可区分，即"token 塌缩"（token collapse），并揭示该失效被后续方法继承；提出评估图-token 对齐的方法而非仅凭任务精度推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图软提示（GNN 学习图特定 token 编码实体关系）被广泛用于将图结构注入 LLM 提示，G-Retriever 更是将图软提示与图的口头化描述结合以增强信号。然而现有工作主要依赖任务精度来推断模型是否真正利用了图信息，缺乏对图 token 是否承载图内容的直接检验，因此需要系统剖析其内部机制。

Method: 在 3 个基准、3 种图编码器、2 个 LLM 系列，以及冻结与 LoRA 适配两种设定下对 G-Retriever 进行系统分析；通过替换实验（用数据集均值 token、其他样本 token、损坏图导出的 token 替换）检验答案级影响；用不访问图的学习常量提示作为对照；并结合干预与解码探针（interventions、decoding probes）区分 token 反映的是问题、答案先验还是被 LLM 实际使用的图内容；另提出两阶段流程以缓解塌缩。

Result: 图 token 在不同输入间几乎无法区分；替换 token 在答案精度上无可观测效应；从不访问图的学习常量提示在三个基准上达到或超过 G-Retriever；塌缩失效被后续方法（即使采用多 token 池化）继承，说明塌缩 token 实际充当通用软提示而非传递图内容。两阶段流程可在多数情况下产生非塌缩 token 并保持有竞争力的性能，但 token 变异性本身并不能证明模型使用了图信息。

Conclusion: G-Retriever 类方法的图 token 主要起通用软提示作用，任务精度不足以证明图-token 对齐。作者主张采用基于替换、干预和解码探针的实用评估流程来检验图 token 是否真正携带并驱动图信息利用，并公开代码以支持复现。

Abstract: Recent methods for injecting graph structure into LLM prompts include graph soft prompting, in which a graph neural network (GNN) learns graph-specific tokens that capture relational information among entities in textual prompts. G-Retriever combines graph soft prompts with a verbalized description of the same graph to strengthen the textual prompt signal. We conduct an in-depth analysis of G-Retriever and uncover a pathological behavior we term token collapse. Across three benchmarks, three graph encoders, two LLM families, and both frozen and LoRA-adapted models, G-Retriever's graph tokens become almost indistinguishable across inputs. We provide evidence that the same failure is inherited by subsequent methods, even with multi-token pooling. Under the standard setup, swapping a token with the dataset mean, a token from another example, or one derived from a corrupted graph produces no resolved answer-level effect in accuracy. Moreover, a learned constant prompt that never accesses the graph matches or surpasses G-Retriever on all three benchmarks. These findings indicate that, rather than conveying graph content as intended, the collapsed token functions primarily as a generic soft prompt. We also examine conditions linked to this collapse and propose a two-stage procedure that yields non-collapsed tokens while maintaining competitive performance in most cases. However, token variability alone does not prove that the model uses graph information: via interventions and decoding probes, we show that depending on the configuration, a token can reflect the question, an answer prior, or graph-derived content that the LLM may or may not use. Overall, our results offer a practical approach to evaluating graph-token alignment rather than inferring it from task accuracy alone. Code to reproduce our experiments is available at https://github.com/difra100/Token_Collapse_KGQA.

</details>


### [125] [Rethinking Sequence Compression with Test-time Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31574)
*Sihan Yang,Jie Huang,Hanxuan Li,Yifan Guo,Weigao Sun,Yiran Zhong,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TTR-Compressor，将序列压缩重新表述为测试时回归问题：输入 token 定义回归问题，其闭式求解的拟合系数即为压缩表示，回归问题本身端到端学习。在块稀疏注意力与多模态视觉 token 合并两任务上均优于池化/投影类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列压缩是高效语言与多模态模型的关键信息瓶颈，但现有基于池化（pooling）或学习投影（learned projection）的方法性能不足，需要更有效的压缩范式。

Method: 以测试时回归视角重构压缩：由输入 token 构造回归问题，前向传播中用闭式解高效计算拟合系数作为压缩表示；回归问题的参数则通过反向传播端到端学习，从而兼顾表达力与计算效率。

Result: 在块稀疏注意力中，TTR-Compressor 在 RULER 基准上大幅超越基于注意力的压缩器；在视觉 token 合并中，持续优于标准 MLP merger，并在减少 55.6% 视觉 token 的情况下达到相当性能。

Conclusion: 测试时回归可作为语言与多模态高效模型中序列压缩的有效基础原语（primitive），为替代池化与投影类压缩提供了通用方案。

Abstract: Sequence compression is a fundamental operation and a critical information bottleneck in efficient language and multimodal models, and yet prevailing approaches based on pooling or learned projections remain underperforming. We revisit sequence compression through the lens of test-time regression: input tokens define a regression problem whose fitted coefficients form the compressed representation. Importantly, the regression problem is learned end-to-end via backpropagation, while its coefficients are computed efficiently in closed form during the forward pass. This leads to TTR-Compressor, a general alternative to conventional pooling and projection-based compression. We evaluate TTR-Compressor as the compressor for block-wise sparse attention and the visual token merger for multimodal large language models. On block-wise sparse attention, TTR-Compressor improves RULER performance over attention-based compressors by a large margin. For visual token merging, it consistently outperforms the standard MLP merger and matches its performance with 55.6% fewer visual tokens. These results establish test-time regression as an effective primitive for sequence compression in efficient language and multimodal models.

</details>


### [126] [Action Learning for Scientific Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31585)
*Sarah Liaw,Yuanqi Du,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“动作学习”（action learning）问题并给出 LEAP 方法：将未知动作锚定到可处理的参考过程，通过比较观测轨迹与参考轨迹学习修正项，在路径空间构造可微目标，从而同时刻画局部动力学与非加性时间依赖，并可将同一动作复用于路径排序/推断、机制引导、约束优化与采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解与控制系统演化需要关于完整轨迹的推理，但现有方法存在两类局限：学习局部动力学或直接生成轨迹的方法，其基于单步转移构造的目标会遗漏跨时间依赖；生成模型又不一定能提供可处理的目标来评估与优化任意路径。因此需要一个统一的、可对轨迹整体进行评估与优化的目标。

Method: 研究动作学习问题：从轨迹观测中恢复随机动作。LEAP（Learned Energy on Action Paths）把未知动作锚定在一个可处理的参考过程上，通过比较观测轨迹与参考轨迹来学习剩余修正项，从而在路径空间上得到可微目标；该构造既能捕捉局部动力学结构，也能表达跨时间的非加性依赖。

Result: 学到的动作可灵活复用于路径排序与推断、机制引导、约束优化与采样。在随机物理系统、多体轨道动力学和分子模拟上，LEAP 能恢复潜在动力学、刻画跨时间依赖，并在不同推断目标与物理条件之间实现迁移。

Conclusion: 动作学习是一个有前景的框架，可将轨迹观测转化为可复用的目标，用于科学推断与控制。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling how systems evolve is central to science and engineering, where many problems require reasoning about complete trajectories. Existing approaches typically learn local dynamics or generate trajectories, but objectives constructed from individual transitions can miss dependencies across time, while generative models do not necessarily provide a tractable objective for evaluating and optimizing arbitrary paths. We study $\textit{action learning}$, the problem of recovering a stochastic action from trajectory observations, yielding a common objective for reasoning about dynamical systems. We introduce $\textbf{LEAP}$ ($\textbf{L}$earned $\textbf{E}$nergy on $\textbf{A}$ction $\textbf{P}$aths), which anchors the unknown action to a tractable reference process and learns the remaining correction by comparing observed trajectories with reference trajectories. This construction yields a differentiable objective on path space that can capture both local dynamical structure and nonadditive dependencies across time. Once learned, the same action can be flexibly reused for path ranking and inference, mechanism steering, constrained optimization and sampling. Across stochastic physical systems, many-body orbital dynamics and molecular simulations, LEAP recovers underlying dynamics, captures dependencies across time and enables transfer across inference objectives and physical conditions. We show that $\textit{action learning}$ is a promising framework for turning trajectory observations into reusable objectives for scientific inference and control.

</details>


### [127] [CRANE: Post-Hoc Counterfactual Retrieval for Multimodal Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31594)
*Franco Rugolon,Ioanna Miliou,Panagiotis Papapetrou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个模型无关、事后式多模态最近邻反事实解释框架CRANE，通过检索真实训练样本作为反事实，并以多模态共识检索（MC-R）作为最强融合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法仅面向单模态（表格、时序、文本、图像），缺乏针对多模态分类器的事后解释方法；现有可解释性方法对多模态分类器仅在各模态上独立施加归因，导致解释不连贯，且无法迁移到任意预训练多模态架构。

Method: CRANE（Counterfactual Retrieval via Agnostic Nearest-neighbour Explanations）：模型无关、事后式框架，从目标类真实训练样本中检索近邻作为候选反事实，从而在构造上保证合理性；并提出多模态共识检索（MC-R），对各模态的邻居集合取交集，以找到在每个模态上都与查询样本接近的实例。

Result: 在四个异质数据集和三种融合策略上评估，MC-R在四个数据集中的三个上取得最优或并列最优结果，且每条解释均扎根于联合训练分布；当单一模态主导决策边界时，单模态策略仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: CRANE为多模态分类器提供了首个模型无关的事后反事实解释方案，基于真实样本检索保证合理性，MC-R是通用性最强的多模态策略，而单模态策略在模态主导情形下仍适用。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanation methods have been introduced for unimodal settings, with dedicated frameworks for tabular, timeseries, text, and image data, yet no post-hoc method has been designed for classifiers that learn from multimodal inputs. Existing explainability methods for multimodal classifiers apply modality-specific attribution independently to each input, which fails to yield coherent explanations and does not transfer to arbitrary pre-trained multimodal architectures. To address these shortcomings, we present CRANE (Counterfactual Retrieval via Agnostic Nearest-neighbour Explanations), the first post-hoc, model-agnostic framework for nearest-neighbour multimodal counterfactual generation that retrieves real training examples from the target class as candidate explanations, ensuring plausibility by construction. Across four heterogeneous datasets and three fusion strategies, we show that Multimodal Consensus Retrieval (MC-R), which intersects per-modality neighbour sets to identify instances jointly close to the query in every modality, emerges as the strongest multimodal strategy, achieving the best or joint-best outcome on three of four datasets while grounding every explanation in the joint training distribution. Unimodal strategies remain competitive when a single modality dominates the decision boundary.

</details>


### [128] [Efficient and Optimal Learning of Discrete Distributions with Person-Level Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31612)
*Gautam Kamath,Mahbod Majid,Ankur Moitra,Argyris Mouzakis,Jonathan Ullman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在批量（batched）设置下研究person-level差分隐私的离散分布学习问题。作者给出纯DP与近似DP下的多项式时间算法：纯(ε,0)-DP达到 n=Õ(k/(α²m)+k/(α√m·ε)+k/ε)，首次在多项式时间内匹配信息论最优（至对数因子）；近似(ε,δ)-DP达到 Õ(k/(α²m)+k/(α√m·ε)+k+log(1/δ)/ε)。算法基于新的6次平方和（SoS）估计器，关键是对"修复后批量均值的经验协方差"与"由均值预测的多项式协方差"之差构造迹有界的对角majorizer，并借助Friedland–Lim加强型对称Grothendieck不等式通过SDP对偶证明干净数据满足该证书。另给出统计查询（SQ）下界，排除固定多项式SQ资源下误差阶数统一更小的可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在person-level差分隐私与批量采样（每用户贡献m个独立样本）场景下，学习[ k ]上离散分布μ的TV误差α，需要刻画达到最优误差所需的用户数n。此前纯DP情形缺乏与信息论下界匹配的多项式时间算法，同时需要对"不可信批量"进行鲁棒估计并把鲁棒率转化为隐私所需的score函数，因此动机是给出紧的样本复杂度与多项式时间可实现算法。

Method: 采用基于6次平方和（degree-6 SoS）的估计器处理不可信批量；核心构件为针对"修复批量均值的经验协方差减去其均值对应的多项式协方差"的迹有界对角majorizer，通过半定规划（SDP）对偶及Friedland–Lim的对称型加强Grothendieck不等式证明干净数据满足该证书；证书保留批量均值的均值依赖方差，从而给出近最优鲁棒率 O(η√(log(1/η)/m)) 并提供隐私归约所需的score；结合纯DP与近似DP的隐私归约得到样本复杂度。另用统计查询（SQ）框架证明固定多项式资源下的下界。

Result: 纯(ε,0)-DP：n=Õ(k/(α²m)+k/(α√m·ε)+k/ε)，为首次多项式时间、与信息论最优仅差对数因子的纯DP保证。近似(ε,δ)-DP：n=Õ(k/(α²m)+k/(α√m·ε)+k+log(1/δ)/ε)；当δ=2^{-Θ(k)}且α,ε,m与失败概率固定时为Õ(k)。鲁棒率 O(η√(log(1/η)/m)) 近最优。SQ下界表明固定多项式SQ资源界下无法一致地减小误差阶。

Conclusion: 批量person-level DP下离散分布学习可在多项式时间内达到（至对数因子）信息论最优的纯DP样本复杂度；6次SoS加迹有界对角majorizer证书、SDP对偶与对称Grothendieck不等式构成关键技术工具，并给出隐私归约所需score；近似DP情形进一步获得δ=2^{-Θ(k)}时的Õ(k)界；SQ下界说明在给定资源框架内该误差阶无法统一改进。

Abstract: We study learning a discrete distribution under person-level differential privacy in the batched setting. Given $n$ users, each contributing $m$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution $μ$ over $[k]$, we seek an estimate of $μ$ with total-variation error $α$. We give polynomial-time algorithms under both pure and approximate differential privacy. Under pure $(\varepsilon,0)$-DP, we achieve $n = \widetilde O(k/(α^2 m)+k/(α\sqrt{m}\varepsilon)+k/\varepsilon)$, the first polynomial-time pure-DP guarantee matching the information-theoretic optimum up to logarithmic factors. Under approximate $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP, we achieve $n = \widetilde O(k/(α^2 m)+k/(α\sqrt{m}\varepsilon)+k+\log(1/δ)/\varepsilon)$. At $δ=2^{-Θ(k)}$, this is $\widetilde O(k)$ for fixed $α,\varepsilon,m$ and failure probability. Our algorithms use a new degree-6 sum-of-squares estimator for learning from untrusted batches. Its key ingredient is a trace-bounded diagonal majorizer for the difference between the empirical covariance of the repaired batch averages and the multinomial covariance predicted by their mean. We prove that clean data admit this certificate through semidefinite-programming duality and a new application of the stronger symmetric form of Grothendieck's inequality due to Friedland and Lim. The certificate preserves the mean-dependent variance of multinomial batch averages, yielding the near-optimal robust rate $O(η\sqrt{\log(1/η)/m})$ and providing the score required by the privacy reduction. We also prove a statistical query lower bound ruling out a uniformly smaller order of error under fixed polynomial SQ resource bounds.

</details>


### [129] [OP-Print: Tracing Membership Imprints in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31616)
*Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个研究on-policy自蒸馏成员推断攻击的工作，发现监督印记稀疏且结构化集中于不确定性敏感修正点，据此提出统一攻击框架OP-Print，在灰盒与黑盒设置下均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy自蒸馏因无需外部教师而实用，但其隐私风险（尤其成员推断）几乎未被探索；同一训练样本的两个耦合视图可能加剧记忆化，促使作者首次系统研究该威胁。

Method: 提出OP-Print统一攻击框架，基于"监督印记稀疏且结构化、集中于不确定性敏感修正位点"的发现构造两类信号：灰盒下直接聚合同一rollout上的特权师生残差修正；黑盒下用基于重启的输出盆地稳定性作为隐藏修正过程的可观测代理。

Result: 在多种模型与蒸馏方案上，所有基线攻击即使在灰盒访问下也基本等同于随机猜测，而OP-Print在灰盒与黑盒设置下均被广泛验证有效。

Conclusion: on-policy自蒸馏并非无隐私风险，其特有的残差修正痕迹可被利用实施成员推断；OP-Print揭示了该风险并提供跨访问级别的统一检测手段。

Abstract: On-policy distillation has been widely adopted for alleviating the distribution mismatch of off-policy distillation. Self-distillation further removes the need for an external teacher by using the student's own outputs, enhanced with extra context or demonstrations, as supervision. This teacher-free design makes on-policy self-distillation practical, yet its privacy risks remain largely unexplored. In particular, using two coupled views of the same training example may strengthen its memorization. Motivated by this, we present the first study of membership inference attacks against on-policy self-distillation. We investigate both gray-box and black-box threat models and propose a unified attack framework, named OP-Print. OP-Print builds on our finding that the supervision imprint in on-policy self-distillation is sparse and structured, concentrating on uncertainty-sensitive correction sites rather than across the entire rollout. Exploiting this property, we construct attack signals tailored to the two access levels: in the gray-box setting, OP-Print directly aggregates the privileged teacher-student residual correction along the same rollout; in the black-box setting, it measures restart-based output-basin stability as an observable surrogate for the hidden correction process. Across diverse models and distillation schemes, all baseline attacks remain essentially at random guessing even under gray-box access, whereas OP-Print is broadly validated to be effective in both gray-box and black-box settings.

</details>


### [130] [Questioning Instrumental Convergence in RLVR: Checkpoint Evidence from OLMo-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31624)
*Barış Alparslan,Sandy Tanwisuth,Caleb Petterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究以checkpoint轨迹视角考察RLVR是否加剧LLM的工具性收敛行为；在OLMo-3-7B-Think上，工具性行为率由SFT的25.0%升至RLVR约200步的46.1%，最终降至11.8%，低于SFT基线，呈非单调模式，且提示框架效应无法解释该差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具性收敛（instrumental convergence）常被用作"有能力智能体会获得权力寻求类子目标（自我保存、资源获取、欺骗、抵抗监督）"的一般性理由，构成对社会的重要威胁。对LLM而言，强化学习是后训练中首个直接针对目标优化行为的阶段，因而在IC论据下最可能出现收敛性工具行为。作者由此提出问题：带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）是否使模型更易表达工具性收敛行为，还是取决于训练机制、评测框架与后训练阶段。

Method: 将该问题建模为以checkpoint为条件的行为轨迹，而非单一的前/后安全分数。使用InstrumentalEval评测OLMo-3-7B-Think，覆盖SFT、DPO、13个中间RLVR检查点及最终RLVR模型；采用配对的项目级检验与逐步回归；控制提示框架影响（有害提示指示变量及其他框架效应的组合仅解释检查点内分数方差的约15%，平均），并用迭代零空间投影（INLP）移除可检测的框架方向；另报告32B端点的初步结果。

Result: 轨迹呈非单调：工具性行为率由SFT的25.0%上升至RLVR约第200步的46.1%，随后在最终检查点降至11.8%。配对项目级检验与逐步回归支持最终的SFT→RLVR下降。框架控制分析显示该模式不能被提示框架解释：INLP移除可检测的框架方向后，SFT到最终RLVR的差距不变。32B端点初步结果同向（SFT 35.53% → RLVR最终22.37%）。

Conclusion: 在该设定下RLVR并不单调提升工具性行为：比率先升后降，最终低于SFT基线。结论仅适用于该模型族与基准，且评判者一致性为中等，绝对比率应谨慎解读。

Abstract: Instrumental convergence is often invoked as a general reason to expect capable agents to acquire or express power-seeking subgoals: self-preservation, resource acquisition, deception, or resistance to oversight which poses an important threat to the society. For LLMs, reinforcement learning is the first stage of post-training that directly optimizes behavior against an objective; therefore, it is the stage where convergent instrumental behavior, on IC grounds, likely to appear. Does reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) make a model more likely to express instrumental-convergence behavior, or does this depend on the training regime, evaluation frame, and stage of post-training? We study this question as a checkpoint-conditioned behavioral trajectory rather than as a single before/after safety score. Using InstrumentalEval, we evaluate OLMo-3-7B-Think across SFT, DPO, 13 intermediate RLVR checkpoints, and the final RLVR model. The trajectory is nonmonotonic: instrumental rate rises from 25.0% at SFT to 46.1% near RLVR step 200, then falls to 11.8% at the final checkpoint. Paired item-level tests and step-wise regression support the final SFT-to-RLVR reduction. Prompt framing does not explain this pattern: a harmful-prompt indicator combined with other framing effects explains only 15% of within-checkpoint score variance on average, and Iterative Null-space Projection (INLP) removes a detectable framing direction without changing the SFT-to-final-RLVR gap. Together with preliminary 32B endpoint results (35.53% SFT to 22.37% RLVR final), these findings indicate that RLVR does not monotonically increase instrumental behavior in this setting: the rate rises early, then falls below the SFT baseline. The result is specific to this model family and benchmark, and judge agreement is moderate, so absolute rates should be read cautiously.

</details>


### [131] [BeNice! Closing Looping's Inference-Memory Gap via KV-Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31633)
*Hayden Prairie,Jacob M. Ryan,Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick,Daniel Y. Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究循环架构中KV-cache可共享性（KV-shareability）的成因，提出两阶段方法（预训练随机深度 + 后训练BeNice!）实现KV共享，在不损失质量的前提下降低长上下文推理显存并提升吞吐与缓存命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环（looped）架构通过重复将激活传入同一组层来扩展质量并固定参数量，但推理时每次循环都生成独立KV-cache，导致显存随循环次数线性增长。已有工作发现某些情况下最终循环的KV-cache可与早期循环共享而无质量损失（KV-shareability），但其机制不明，且同等训练的循环模型并不稳定具备该性质。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：第一阶段在预训练中使用随机深度（stochastic depth），诱导收缩型残差动力学（contractive residual dynamics），使表示在多次循环间趋向共同极限；第二阶段提出后训练方法BeNice!，将最终循环的KV-cache广播到第一次循环，训练其产生兼容的残差，同时保持原模型质量。

Result: BeNice!在实现KV共享的同时达到与标准微调（不共享）相当的验证困惑度。KV共享与其他KV压缩方法互补：可与内存高效序列混合器（如multi-latent attention、Kimi delta attention）及低精度KV-cache量化（fp8、mxfp8、nvfp4）组合。在模拟低显存长上下文推理中，KV共享使缓存命中率最高提升31.4%，吞吐最高提升23.6%，p90首token时延（TTFT）最高降低83.6%。

Conclusion: KV-shareability可通过"收缩残差动力学"的预训练设计与兼容性后训练（BeNice!）被主动引导获得；该方法无质量损失、与现有KV压缩技术正交可组合，并在长上下文低显存推理场景带来显著效率收益。

Abstract: Looped architectures scale quality while keeping parameter count fixed by repeatedly passing activations through the same block of layers. During inference, each recurrence generates independent KV-caches, causing memory to grow linearly. To address this challenge, prior work has proposed KV-sharing (i.e., sharing KV-caches across depth), finding that, in some cases, the KV-cache of the final recurrence can be shared with the earlier recurrences without quality loss, a property we denote as KV-shareability. However, the mechanisms underlying KV-shareability remain poorly understood, with even identically trained looped architectures not consistently exhibiting it. In this paper, we study how KV-shareability can be enabled in looped models. We propose a two-stage method: in the first stage, we use stochastic depth during pretraining, which induces contractive residual dynamics that drive representations toward a common limit across recurrences. In the second stage, we propose BeNice!, a post-training method that broadcasts the final recurrence's KV-cache into the first recurrence, training it to produce compatible residuals while preserving the original model's quality. As a result, BeNice! enables KV-shareability while matching standard finetuning's unshared validation perplexity. Using BeNice!, we show that KV-sharing is complementary to alternative KV-compression methods, composing with memory-efficient sequence mixers (e.g., multi-latent attention, Kimi delta attention) and low-precision KV-cache quantization (e.g., fp8, mxfp8, nvfp4). Finally, in simulated low-memory, long-context inference, KV-sharing improves cache hit rates by up to 31.4%, increases throughput by up to 23.6%, and reduces p90 time-to-first-token by up to 83.6%.

</details>


### [132] [Causal Chain of Thought: Mitigating Attention Leakage via Context Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31639)
*Gabriele Dominici,Teague McMillan,Fazl Barez,Marc Langheinrich,Martin Gjoreski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CausalCoT 推理框架，通过将问题分解为原子子问题的有向无环图，并在仅有直接因果父节点的隔离上下文中评估每个节点，使中间推理步骤对最终答案具有真实因果敏感性，在保持预测性能的同时显著提升解释忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归生成中，稠密注意力使每个推理步骤都能访问完整上下文，模型可能通过直接查表得出答案，再生成看似合理但实为事后辩护的推理链。现有 CoT 的步骤并不能保证因果驱动最终答案，即解释缺乏忠实性。

Method: CausalCoT 将问题分解为原子子问题的有向无环图（DAG），对每个节点仅在包含其直接因果父节点的隔离上下文中独立评估，形成马尔可夫式结构，从而切断指向非父节点内容的注意力通路，使下游预测依赖局部推理而非全局上下文泄漏；该依赖图还支持测试时干预，使局部修正可沿图可预测地向下游传播。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，CausalCoT 保持有竞争力的预测性能，同时提升解释忠实度，中间推理步骤的因果敏感性相比最强基线平均提高 12 个百分点，最高提升 21 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过带因果父节点隔离的结构化分解，可在不牺牲性能的前提下实现因果透明的推理，并为测试时的局部干预与可控纠错提供可预测的传播机制。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting elicits step-by-step rationales that appear to explain model predictions, but there is no guarantee these steps causally drive the final answer. In standard autoregressive generation, dense attention allows each reasoning step to attend to the full context, enabling the model to derive answers through direct lookup while generating plausible-sounding but post-hoc justifications. We introduce CausalCoT, an inference framework that enforces causal transparency by decomposing problems into a directed acyclic graph of atomic sub-questions, evaluating each node in an isolated context containing only its direct causal parents. This Markovian structure removes attention pathways to non-parent content, making downstream predictions sensitive to local reasoning rather than global context leakage. Across a diverse suite of reasoning benchmarks, CausalCoT preserves competitive predictive performance while improving explanation faithfulness, increasing causal sensitivity of intermediate reasoning steps by an average of 12 and up to 21 percentage points over the strongest baseline. The structured dependency graph additionally enables test-time interventions in which local corrections propagate predictably through downstream reasoning.

</details>


### [133] [A Fix for Softmax and Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31642)
*Jonathan T. Barron,Dan Gnanapragasam,Michael Tschannen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Stand中有两个数值bug：Softmax Collapse（目标logit远大于其他时其梯度被舍入为0，梯度不再求和为零）与Softmax Cancellation（师生logit接近时即使相等也产生非零梯度）。作者给出修复二者的简洁log-softmax/softmax/交叉熵实现，并展示其修正多重训练现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准softmax、log-softmax与交叉熵实现存在两处数值缺陷，会破坏梯度正确性并引发一系列训练异常（Slingshot现象、grokking变慢、无权重衰减时精度崩塌、蒸馏中学生漂移）。作者希望从实现层面根除这些病态行为。

Method: 分析标准实现在数值上的两处缺陷来源，提出简单的log-softmax、softmax与交叉熵实现来同时修复Softmax Collapse（目标logit过大时梯度被舍入为零、梯度和不为零）和Softmax Cancellation（师生logit接近或相等时出现虚假非零梯度）。

Result: 修复后：消除了此前观察到的"Slingshot"现象；配合权重衰减加速grokking；无权重衰减时防止精度崩塌；蒸馏中学生网络不再偏离教师；并表明记忆之后logit增长受权重衰减与Adam的ε超参数之间的平衡所约束。

Conclusion: softmax相关数值bug是可修复的实现问题，修复后带来一致的训练改善；同时揭示了logit增长的上界由权重衰减与Adam ε的平衡决定，为理解记忆后训练动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: Standard implementations of softmax, log-softmax, and cross-entropy have two numerical bugs. The first bug, Softmax Collapse, rounds the target logit's gradient to zero if that one logit is much larger than the others, resulting in gradients that no longer sum to zero. The second bug, Softmax Cancellation, occurs when student logits are close to teacher logits, and produces non-zero gradients even when the student and teacher logits are equal. We present simple implementations of log-softmax, softmax, and cross-entropy that fix both bugs. Our fix eliminates the previously-observed "Slingshot" phenomenon, speeds up grokking with weight decay, prevents accuracy collapse without weight decay, stops student networks from drifting away from the teacher during distillation, and shows that after memorization, logit growth is bounded by an equilibrium between weight decay and Adam's $ε$ hyperparameter.

</details>


### [134] [METTA: Memory-Enhanced Test-Time Adaptation for Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31656)
*Ali Najar,Hosein Hasani,Amir Kooshan Fattah,Yasamin Rajabi,Mahdieh Soleymani Baghshah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出METTA（Memory-Enhanced Test-Time Adaptation），通过双时间尺度记忆结构（因果神经记忆做episode内细粒度适应、紧凑episode级摘要做跨episode任务推断），以线性复杂度实现长上下文元强化学习的高效快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元强化学习要求智能体从有限交互中快速适应未见任务，但需要在长交互历史上表征任务证据。经典循环智能体将经验压缩进隐状态，能力受限；Transformer类智能体需对完整轨迹做注意力，内存与计算代价随序列长度呈二次增长，难以扩展到长上下文。

Method: 将快速适应阶段分解为两个时间尺度：（1）因果神经记忆（causal neural memory）在episode内部执行细粒度的在线适应，序列复杂度为线性；（2）紧凑的episode级摘要支撑跨episode的长程任务推断，避免对完整历史做注意力。同时神经记忆提供足够表达能力与测试时训练能力，支持在元测试阶段适应未见过的held-out任务族。

Result: 在未见过的Meta-World ML10/45测试环境中，成功率超过50%，且训练预算低、wall-clock时间短；在长上下文稀疏奖励的PointMaze任务上，相较Transformer基线展现出更具结构化的探索行为并取得更优表现。

Conclusion: METTA为更具适应性、可扩展的长上下文元强化学习提供了一条高效路径，说明分层记忆（局部因果记忆+全局episode摘要）可替代全序列注意力，在保证表达能力的同时显著降低长上下文的计算与内存开销。

Abstract: Meta-reinforcement learning aims to train agents that adapt quickly to unseen tasks from limited interaction, but this requires representing task evidence over long interaction histories. Classical recurrent agents compress experience into hidden states, while transformer-based agents retrieve from past trajectories at quadratic memory and computation cost. In this work, we propose Memory-Enhanced Test-Time Adaptation (METTA) for meta-reinforcement learning. It decomposes the fast adaptation phase into two temporal scales: a causal neural memory performs fine-grained within-episode adaptation with linear sequence complexity, while compact episode-level summaries support long-range cross-episode task inference without attending over the full interaction history. Moreover, equipping agents with neural memory provides sufficient expressive capacity and test-time training capability, enabling effective adaptation to unseen held-out tasks and task families at meta-test time. On unseen held-out Meta-World ML10/45 test environments, METTA achieves a success rate over $50\%$ while maintaining a low training budget and short wall-clock time. On long-context sparse-reward PointMaze tasks, it outperforms transformer-based baselines by exhibiting more structured exploration behavior. These results show that METTA provides an efficient path toward more adaptive and scalable long-context meta-RL.

</details>


### [135] [Cost-Aware Abstention for LLM-Based IT Ticket Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31657)
*Sachin Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"成本感知弃权"形式化为离线、成本条件的就绪性评估框架：在标定数据上按显式成本选择路由策略，冻结后在留出测试集上一次性评估。在47,837条工单、八类语料上，TF-IDF+逻辑回归在阈值0.60下把测试成本从0.763降至0.472（相对降38.1%），错分率由15.3%降至3.5%；提示式LLM表现明显落后，且等渗重标定只改善ECE而不改善判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工单自动路由的误分代价可能高于人工分诊，因此需要"何时弃权、转人工"的成本感知决策；但现有研究往往缺少在显式成本下、对冻结策略做一次性留出评估的规范流程，也常把标定（ECE）与判别（AUROC）混为一谈。

Method: 构建离线评估框架：在标定集上按成本(c_ok,c_triage,c_wrong)=(0,1,5)选择路由策略（含阈值弃权），冻结后在留出测试集上评估。对比四个训练型非LLM分类器与四个LLM（零样本/少样本提示）。采用配对比率自助法置信区间、等渗重标定、事后成本参数敏感性分析，并引入"仅LLM一致性"对照与margin/entropy拒绝基线，同时报告correctness AUROC、tie-aware risk-coverage面积与ECE。

Result: TF-IDF+逻辑回归（阈值0.60，标定集选定）使测试成本0.763→0.472（相对降38.1%，95% CI [35.7%,40.4%]），错分率15.3%→3.5%。在16个成本参数设置中14个优于always-route与always-defer。分层1,000条子集上，最强提示配置准确率0.477，微调DistilBERT为0.879。等渗重标定降低ECE但不提升判别：GPT-4o-mini少样本重标定前后仅一个置信值，correctness AUROC=0.500。解析盈亏平衡概率0.80处，七个提示配置全部弃权，Haiku少样本仅路由19/1,000。

Conclusion: 成本感知的标定-冻结-留出评估能有效降低路由成本，但小模型微调显著优于提示式LLM；标定质量不能代替判别能力，应同时报告AUROC与风险-覆盖率面积。结论限于该数据集、提示设计、模型端点与示例成本，本文是评估框架而非新弃权算法或通用部署认证。

Abstract: Wrong automatic routing of an IT support ticket can cost more than human triage. We evaluate cost-aware abstention as an offline, cost-conditional readiness assessment: select a routing policy on calibration data under explicit costs, then evaluate that frozen policy once on held-out test data. On a public 47,837-ticket, eight-class corpus, we compare four trained non-LLM classifiers with four large language models under zero- and few-shot prompting. Under the illustrative cost model (c_ok, c_triage, c_wrong) = (0, 1, 5), TF-IDF with logistic regression and a threshold of 0.60 selected on calibration reduces test cost from 0.763 to 0.472, a 38.1% relative reduction (paired ratio-bootstrap 95% CI [35.7%, 40.4%]), while lowering wrong routes from 15.3% to 3.5%. Frozen calibration-selected policies also beat both always-route and always-defer on held-out test data in 14 of 16 settings in a post-hoc cost-parameter analysis. The strongest prompted configuration reaches 0.477 accuracy on a stratified 1,000-ticket test subset, versus 0.879 for fine-tuned DistilBERT on the same tickets. Isotonic lowers expected calibration error without reliably improving discrimination: GPT-4o-mini few-shot has one confidence value before and after recalibration and correctness AUROC 0.500. We therefore report correctness AUROC and tie-aware risk-coverage area alongside ECE. At the analytically derived break-even probability 0.80, seven prompted configurations defer all tickets; Haiku few-shot routes only 19/1,000. An LLM-only agreement control and margin/entropy rejection baselines further separate calibration from discrimination. The findings are scoped to this dataset, prompt design, model endpoints, and illustrative costs; the work is an evaluation framework, not a new abstention algorithm or universal deployment certification.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [The Cost of Saying Hello: An Empirical Study of Handshake Overhead in AI Agent Communication Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30151)
*Pranav Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对三大智能体通信协议（MCP 的 2025 initialize 与 2026 discover 两个时代、A2A、ACP）的握手过程进行实证性、分阶段拆解的基准测量，发现往返次数是握手开销的主导因素，且当由 LLM 读取对端元数据时，握手成本还随对端能力清单规模线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体通信协议综述虽将握手开销列为效率指标，但均未给出实测数据；本文旨在填补这一空白，量化握手阶段的真实成本构成。

Method: 基于各协议官方 SDK 构建测量框架，对每个请求采集往返次数、分阶段时延、线上字节数与 token 成本；覆盖冷启动、热重复、能力不匹配、元数据降级四类场景，每格 N=50，在回环与模拟 50 ms RTT 两种网络条件下测试。

Result: 往返次数主导握手成本：旧版 MCP 握手需 3 次往返、50 ms RTT 下耗时 230 ms；A2A 仅需 1 次往返、61.0 ms。当 LLM 读取对端元数据时，握手成本随能力清单规模增长（每项能力 24–107 token），200 项能力的对端在读取自身任务前即占用 200k 上下文窗口的 11%。MCP 2026 修订版将握手由 3 次往返降至 2 次，配合时代固定（era pinning）在热重连时降为 0 次。

Conclusion: 握手开销由往返次数与元数据 token 成本共同决定，协议演化（MCP 2026）本身印证了所测成本；作者开源完整测量框架、原始数据与分析，为协议设计优化提供可复现依据。

Abstract: Every interaction between AI agents begins with a handshake: the initiating agent must locate its counterpart, learn what it can do, and establish a session before the first useful message can be sent. Surveys of agent communication protocols name handshake overhead as an efficiency criterion, yet none report measurements. This paper presents the first empirical, phase-decomposed benchmark of the handshake in three major agent protocols: MCP (in both its 2025 initialize and 2026 discover eras), A2A, and ACP. Using each protocol's official SDK, we instrument every request for round trips, per-phase latency, wire bytes, and token cost across cold-start, warm-repeat, capability-mismatch, and degraded-metadata scenarios (N=50 per cell, on loopback and under an emulated 50 ms RTT). Round-trip count dominates handshake cost. A legacy MCP handshake takes three round trips and 230 ms at 50 ms RTT; A2A needs one round trip and 61.0 ms. Round trips are not the whole bill: when an LLM reads the counterpart's metadata, handshake cost scales with the counterpart's capability inventory at 24-107 tokens per capability, so a 200-capability counterpart spends 11% of a 200k-token context window before the agent reads its own task. MCP's own 2026 revision cuts its handshake from three round trips to two and, with era pinning, to zero on warm reconnects, protocol evolution that corroborates the cost we measure. We release the full harness, raw data, and analysis at https://github.com/pvarshh/handshake-benchmark.

</details>


### [137] [MCP vs CLI for AI Agents: A Comparative Analysis of Tool Integration Strategies for Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30157)
*Arun Malik,Nitish Talasu,Bradley Klemick,Vineet Tushar Trivedi,Prachi Shah,Sanay Devi,Junjie Zhang,Kunal Sonawane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文对比AI智能体的两种接口范式CLI与MCP，基于14个评估维度、安全评估与经济建模，指出CLI在单用户本地场景更高效，MCP在细粒度访问控制、凭证隔离、结构化审计与多租户治理上不可替代，并说明渐进式披露可消除MCP的上下文开销劣势，推荐生产系统采用混合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体需通过接口与文件、API、数据库、云服务等外部系统交互，当前CLI（直接执行shell命令）与MCP（基于JSON-RPC的受治理、类型化工具访问协议）两种范式并存且相互竞争，但缺乏系统性对比，业界对二者适用边界与安全、经济权衡认识不足。

Method: 构建14个维度的比较分析框架，辅以具体案例（worked examples）；依据NSA正式指南（CSI U/OO/6030316-26）、OWASP安全标准等合规框架开展安全评估；结合BCG与Gartner的经济模型进行成本分析；并考察渐进式披露（progressive disclosure）技术对MCP上下文窗口开销的改善。

Result: CLI在单用户、纯本地工作流中效率更优；MCP可满足CLI本质上无法实现的需求，包括细粒度访问控制、凭证隔离、结构化审计与多租户治理；渐进式披露使MCP的token效率达到与CLI相当的水平，同时保留其架构优势。

Conclusion: CLI与MCP并非简单替代关系，而各有适用边界；生产系统宜采用混合架构，在保留CLI效率优势的同时利用MCP的治理与安全能力。分析结论有学术、政府与产业多方来源支撑。

Abstract: AI agents require interfaces to interact with external systems such as files, APIs, databases, and cloud services. Two competing paradigms have emerged: CLI (Command Line Interface), where agents execute shell commands directly, and MCP (Model Context Protocol), a structured JSON-RPC protocol providing governed, typed tool access. This paper presents a comparative analysis across 14 evaluation dimensions, supported by worked examples, security assessment against established compliance frameworks, and economic modeling. Our analysis indicates that CLI provides superior efficiency for single-user, local-only workflows, while MCP addresses requirements that CLI fundamentally cannot satisfy, including fine-grained access control, credential isolation, structured audit, and multi-tenant governance. We further examine how progressive disclosure techniques eliminate MCP's historical weakness (context window overhead), yielding token efficiency comparable to CLI while preserving its architectural benefits. A hybrid architecture is recommended for production systems. This analysis draws upon academic, government, and industry sources including NSA formal guidance (CSI U/OO/6030316-26), OWASP security standards, and economic modeling from BCG and Gartner.

</details>


### [138] [Accelerating Wildfire Hazard Mapping Across Canada with an AI Surrogate Modelling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30170)
*Hager Radi Abdelwahed,Toluwaleke Olutayo,Chirag Goel,Jana Osea,Aldo Zaimi,Tom Swystun,Sandy Erni,Francisco Emiliano Lopez Saavedra,Yan Boulanger,Gaétan Marceau Caron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于多任务深度学习的AI代理模型，替代计算密集的BurnP3+过程式野火模拟，实现火烧概率、平均火强度和蔓延速率的高效预测，获得约806倍CPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加拿大野火活动加剧，亟需全国性灾害制图以支持燃料管理、土地利用规划、基础设施保护和疏散路线设计。但BurnP3/BurnP3+等过程式框架依赖计算昂贵的蒙特卡洛模拟，单个区域建模单元需数天至数周，构成计算瓶颈。
